# OpenClaw v2026.10.1

AI agents and tools can read these release notes as [plain Markdown](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/openclaw/openclaw/main/CHANGELOG/2026.10.1.md).

OpenClaw v2026.10.1 helps your agent [find a relevant installed skill](/tools/skills#search-installed-skills) when a task needs it, without putting the whole skill library in the prompt. You can then work with compatible, opt-in [MCP Apps](/cli/mcp/apps#plugin-extensions) beside chat, using supported forms and workspace files. To keep your setup recoverable, [full backups](/install/backups) can now live on external storage or Cloudflare R2 and restore into a fresh staging directory.

[New and expanded features](#new-and-expanded-features) · [Fixes and refinements](#fixes-and-refinements)

## New and expanded features

### Installed-skill discovery

Your agent can use [installed-skill search](/tools/skills#search-installed-skills) to find a relevant skill when a task needs it, without loading the whole skill library into the conversation. This works in supported embedded agents, Code Mode and [Codex sessions](/plugins/codex-harness/runtime-behavior).

Searching instruction text follows the agent's current read permission and may cover only part of a file, so no match does not mean no suitable skill exists. The agent reads a selected skill's full instructions separately; that read grants no permission to install or run it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Eligible installed skills remain discoverable when the prompt's size limit leaves them out of its skill list. Disabled skills and those unavailable for model use remain excluded. [#158089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158089). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@eltonwf-del](https://github.com/eltonwf-del).
- Installed-skill search returns matching names and descriptions, while an exact-name read returns complete instructions or an explicit size error. New instruction reads support files up to 256 KiB. [#158090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158090). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@eltonwf-del](https://github.com/eltonwf-del) and [@Jarvis-Baba](https://github.com/Jarvis-Baba).
- Embedded agents, Code Mode and Codex gain the installed-skill search and read tools. Codex reads use the correct workspace or delivered sandbox copy and return complete instructions or an error. [#158091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158091). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@eltonwf-del](https://github.com/eltonwf-del).
- Search can find capabilities described inside skill instructions under the agent's current native read permission. Removing that permission also suppresses cached or in-progress instruction matches. [#160538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160538). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Agents receive guidance to search for task-relevant skills even when their prompt contains no skill list. Simple conversation can skip discovery, and known skill names can be read directly. [#162842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162842). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Exact capitalization takes priority when matching skill names, so a request for `Deploy` can distinguish it from `deploy`. [#162849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162849). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Long local instruction files contribute their beginning to search instead of losing all instruction-text matches. Coverage remains partial, with up to 16 KiB per file within the total search budget. Words beyond that prefix are not matched. [#162856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162856). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Discovery guidance also works when filtering leaves only notices or an empty skill list. Existing notices remain visible, and the guidance reflects the tools the agent is allowed to use. [#162963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162963). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- If selected instructions and supporting files exceed the combined 8 MiB limit, the agent receives an explanation to select fewer skills even when diagnostics are off. [#164012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164012). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

</details>

### MCP Apps with conversations and files

With [MCP Apps](/cli/mcp/apps#plugin-extensions) enabled and a compatible server configured, you can work in an app beside chat and attach selected content to your next message. Supported apps can also view or edit workspace files and offer richer forms with previews and resource choices.

Messages and file opens show their approval controls in the app pane, with temporary approval available for repeated use of one app tool. File editors can overwrite a newer edit if they do not check for stale saves. [Native Codex forms](/plugins/codex-harness/native-features#rich-mcp-forms) need a prompting approval policy.

![The Bits & Bolts reference MCP app beside a conversation, with selected app content available to attach](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/f23f92a3-d096-4c24-8a16-422dd9329245)

_Reference-app demo with sample workspace data, supplied in [#161747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161747)._

App panels fill the available space in fullscreen and split layouts, and transparent apps that follow the host theme match light and dark dashboards. File views update after edits, and dashboard content reflects accepted board changes.

<details>
<summary>Changes and sources</summary>

- Open apps directly, use their settings, attach removable content, and view or edit advertised workspace-file formats. [#161747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161747). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- File views follow appended or replaced files, consumed context clears without losing newer selections, and inactive panels explain how to restore interaction. [#164277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164277). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- App messages, workspace file opens and confirmed board prompts use visible approval controls inside their pane. [#164493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164493). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeated calls to one app tool can share a temporary approval for the current view. [#164496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164496). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tool and resource-template listings preserve server pagination tokens, including empty and whitespace-only tokens, to retrieve the correct next page. [#164368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164368). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- MCP session controls follow current metadata, withdrawn previews disappear, and access revoked during preparation returns an authorization error. [#163548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163548). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Transparent, theme-aware MCP Apps blend with the dashboard in light and dark modes; apps with their own opaque background retain it. [#161494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161494). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Dashboard, widget and MCP App reads follow previously accepted board writes, avoiding older widget revisions. A changed destination rejects the read instead of serving from its former location. [#164448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164448). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- MCP Apps fill their dashboard panel again in fullscreen, resized and split layouts instead of shrinking above a blank gap. [11515ce](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/11515ce5b3dad2dde1c8a667c6252f62085dd6de). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

### Backups on external storage

You can keep full backups on an external drive, mounted storage or Cloudflare R2 and restore them into a fresh staging directory before activation. Scheduled backups are opt-in, with optional retention that removes eligible older copies after a successful backup and keeps the newest one.

Choose a passphrase for encrypted uploads or explicitly choose `none` for plaintext. Destination names and markers stay visible, and `--output` keeps a plaintext local archive even when the upload is encrypted. Keep the passphrase, marker and namespace for recovery. Give running installations separate namespaces, and take over an existing one only after its previous owner stops. Restore settings, databases and credentials from one matching backup generation.

Follow the guides for initializing [backup destinations](/cli/storage), configuring [storage locations](/concepts/storage-locations) and [Cloudflare R2](/plugins/cloudflare), and [backup and restore](/install/backups).

Backup and storage controls also gain translated status and setup guidance in the existing locales.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Full backups support external storage and Cloudflare R2, optional schedules and retention, destination checks and restoration into fresh staging. [#161913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161913). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Full restore checks archive size plus a 256 MiB free-space reserve before extraction when destination capacity can be measured. [#164492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164492). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@smoe](https://github.com/smoe).
- Restore guides explain extra extraction space and recovering configuration, databases and credentials from one matching backup generation. [#164491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164491). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Backup and storage controls, status and provider or encryption guidance use the existing translated locales. [#162578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162578).

</details>

### Code Mode working values

In interactive Code Mode, your agent can save small JSON results and reuse them in later cells, follow-up replies and after a restart. Only completed cells save their data, and ordinary cell variables still start fresh.

**Overwriting or deleting a value leaves older versions in conversation history and exports.** Saved values have size limits, while their accumulated history can grow beyond those limits. If saving reports a warning, check the transcript before assuming it succeeded. See the [session store guide](/tools/code-mode/guest-api#session-store).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Save small JSON working values across interactive Code Mode cells, replies and restarts. Only completed cells save them; older versions remain in conversation history after overwrite or deletion. [#164086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164086). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Wait for Code Mode workers when their Gateway or CLI host shuts down, while another live host retains its own reusable workers. The shutdown ordering applies after restart. [#162141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162141). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

### Conversation snooze

You can [snooze](/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) an eligible conversation in the web Control UI to clear it from the Active sidebar while its work continues. Find it in Snoozed or All and wake it yourself, or let it return at the chosen time or earlier after interaction, completion or pinning. Archived, child and protected conversations are excluded.

![The conversation menu shows Snooze with five wake-time presets](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/2afdd1c3-7823-4fea-a41c-122680bd733c)

_Control UI demonstration with sample conversations, supplied in [#161960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161960)._

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Snooze hides eligible active web conversations until a chosen time while work continues. Snoozed and All views, Wake and Undo make them accessible; interaction, completion or pinning can wake them early. Native controls and archived, child or protected conversations are excluded. [#161960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161960). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

### Bundled desktop setup

Fresh packaged Mac app setup can run OpenClaw through This Mac without installing Node or a separate CLI. It stops with the app by default. Enable **Keep OpenClaw running when the app is closed** to keep channels and automations running in the background; a paused Gateway stays paused.

Eligible older app-managed Node installations can move to the bundled runtime; independent installations and pinned runtimes keep their own setup. Save any settings held only in the service definition to profile configuration or `.env` before retrying a switch. Installs using the current bundled CLI refuse to overwrite a newer service or runtime choice; inspect it before retrying. See the [bundled Gateway guide](/platforms/mac/bundled-gateway).

Fresh Linux companion setups also use bundled Bun. Existing installations switch only when you confirm **Use bundled runtime…** in the tray, with the Gateway running first. A failed switch needs manual recovery, with no automatic rollback. See [Linux runtime adoption](/platforms/linux#adopt-the-bundled-runtime) and [Bun compatibility](/install/bun-compatibility), including how to return to Node.

![Mac setup page showing the included Gateway and an unchecked option to keep OpenClaw running after the app closes](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/73ff2628-f287-4066-8b35-6d82c9979298)

_Mac setup demonstration with sample state, supplied in [#161779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161779) by [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete)._

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- The Mac private worker and browser setup use bundled Bun and SQLite support without separate Node or Homebrew SQLite. See [Mac app signing](/platforms/mac/signing). [#161603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161603). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fresh This Mac setup starts the included Gateway for the app lifetime, while eligible existing installations retain their ownership. [#161709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161709). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Mac startup dependency-target mismatch is corrected while the macOS 15 minimum remains. [#161754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161754). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@coygeek](https://github.com/coygeek).
- Mac app-managed hosting gains the app-closed background toggle and eligible Node migration and paused-service recovery. [#161779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161779). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

### Model discovery and live catalog updates

Model choices and prices can update without restarting OpenClaw, at a catalog check or after an explicit model-list refresh. Work already underway keeps its original model information and prices. If a downloaded catalog contains incorrect choices or prices, disable remote catalog refresh until a corrected newer catalog is available.

OpenAI API-key users can select supported chat models their account lists before those models reach the bundled catalog. These entries use conservative, text-only limits, and an omitted cost means the price is unknown. Model-list fixes also reduce repeated preparation and duplicated data, keep DeepInfra discovery working with unexpected metadata, and prevent overflowing Vercel prices from appearing as infinite costs.
