- Sidebar Reset restores visible Display settings alongside Filters, while preserving hidden all-agent preferences. [#161437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161437). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- New conversations avoid repeated background reads when their parent is missing while showing the new row and slash commands promptly. [#161442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161442). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Feature-reference navigation restores its existing anchor and removes the retired Tasks entry. [#161453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161453). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Collapsed completed work keeps widgets and browser previews in their execution order. [#161473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161473). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Confirmed non-Git folders hide checkout controls, and switching from cloud or device storage to local does not carry forced isolation. A failed Git check requires a successful retry before creation. [#161475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161475). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation image previews gain faint borders that follow the theme. [#161478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161478). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- System and danger notices have balanced spacing in desktop and touch conversations. [#161479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161479). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Chat activity uses tool icons with exact names on hover or through assistive technology, while expansion keeps the details and outcomes available. [#161483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161483). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The permission picker uses a compact icon with a named menu, tooltip and accessible label. [#161484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161484). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Open person cards retain up to three already-loaded accessible conversation links, keeping their targets steady while times and presence update. Removed entries do not refill until the card is reopened. [#161493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161493). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Screenshot-only recap updates no longer leave a misleading omitted-messages notice. Genuine omitted messages still show the notice. [#161518](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161518). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Assignment failures display the Gateway error, and the current owner is checked and disabled in the picker. [#161562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161562). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible same-origin HTTPS still screenshots can use a smaller preview while opening keeps the original. Animated images and failed or larger conversions keep the original preview. [#161602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161602). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Short history pages avoid parsing older messages that are already indexed. [#161638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161638). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Initial Control UI loading makes fewer JavaScript requests, with remote desktop available when opened. [#161648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161648). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Signed-in startup avoids premature sign-in downloads and duplicate matching self-avatar requests. Authentication rejection still loads the sign-in view. [#161649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161649). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- GitHub hovercards bound their upstream wait and can omit optional pictures or coauthors. Slow required information offers a retryable unavailable result; a partial card may need refresh. [GitHub guide](/plugins/github). [#161653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161653). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Branch statistics include untracked text files whose names start with whitespace. [#161723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161723). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Returning to Live activity restores the active tool filter, including after an older unselected tool disappears. [#161860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161860). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- IP-location labels recover when their component returns, ignore late detached results and clear when the address disappears. [#161911](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161911). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Switching through large rosters avoids unnecessary archive lookups for ordinary and restored conversations. [#161995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161995). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Long-chat local saving reduces repeated serialization and uses idle time when available, with immediate saving on lifecycle changes. [#162004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162004). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Live conversation updates avoid an unrelated display-refresh wait. Slow subscriptions gain [diagnostic phase logs](/gateway/logging). A scoped restoration check lets [Windows update cleanup](/cli/update/how-updates-run) proceed after interruption; it cannot repair an older update already in progress. [#162013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162013). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Typing, streaming and scrolling through loaded long conversations avoid repeated history processing. [#162035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162035). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large-session views and long chats reduce repeated local run checks and saved-stream selection while keeping Stop available. [#162092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162092). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Confirmed Archive, Restore and Undo update the loaded sidebar immediately, without waiting for a separate event or page reload. [#162208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162208). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Model choices distinguish configured Codex alternatives with a codex suffix when both runtimes are offered. A single runtime keeps its plain label. [Model guide](/concepts/models). [#162292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162292). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening the Control UI no longer downloads as much Review display code before it is needed. [#162294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162294). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- System busyness expand and minimize animations survive unrelated updates and still respond to real size changes. [#162315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162315). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation titles no longer gain repeated translated prefixes when those prefixes contain punctuation, and hiding the prefix works too. [#162331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162331). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cmd+K on Apple devices or Ctrl+K elsewhere opens the command palette from a normal-chat dashboard widget. Escape returns to the same input, caret and draft. [#162369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162369). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Cold New Session loading downloads less initial JavaScript and makes fewer requests. [#162382](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162382). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Existing translations cover import results, admin access to folders outside the workspace and catalog-check guidance. [#162451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162451).
- Saved OpenAI safety refusals that contain only an older code now display their explanation and guidance beside a partial answer. [#162469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162469). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An eligible web-chat attempt that stalls before answering can make one recovery attempt in the same conversation. Failure leaves retry guidance; personal or workspace skill-write permission requires a fresh user message. [Queue and recovery guide](/concepts/queue). [#162490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162490). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- The browser terminal keeps its pending output and reports the original cleanup error. [#162520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162520). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Chat controls and viewer avatars avoid redundant updates while real content, access and presence changes still refresh them. [#162536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162536). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tooltips create their popup on first use and reuse it while keeping keyboard focus and accessible descriptions available. [#162539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162539). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large conversations and lists avoid unnecessary styling work and measure offscreen labels when they first become visible. [#162589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162589). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Switching, scrolling and resizing chats avoid repeated layout work while preserving reading position and end anchoring. [#162617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162617). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Simultaneous clients have more bounded waiting capacity for setup and session requests. Overloaded Gateways can still refuse requests. [#162668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162668). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- If both the original and recovery attempts stall before output, Please try again returns instead of leaving the conversation stuck. [#162713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162713). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Codex reasoning and compaction activity no longer appear as empty completed tool cards. Genuine tool activity remains visible. [#162758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162758). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@DarkJuanFra](https://github.com/DarkJuanFra) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Opening Approvals for the first time avoids initializing unrelated chat and redaction work. [#162808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162808). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening the model picker focuses its search without disturbing the unsent conversation draft. [#162832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162832). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- Local workspace icons load without opening chat and can recover after cache eviction, falling back to a folder icon after bounded retries. [#162885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162885). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Offscreen sidebar indicators pause their animation while sessions continue working. [#162905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162905). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The first stored conversation after startup can load sooner. [#162936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162936). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session-only users avoid catalog permission errors, and revoked or late catalog results stop appearing as current. [#162944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162944). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- GitHub icons stay beside the first line of wrapped labels in narrow and right-to-left layouts. [#163047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163047). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Hidden retained chat panes pause updates and apply them on return, while visible split panes continue updating. [#163073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163073). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Browser selections saved before July 1 require reselection, with conversations preserved. An old token alone is not restored as a credential. [#163083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163083). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Older open tabs wait for retained UI-file inventory after a Gateway restart. Tabs whose files have been pruned still need to reload. [#163090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163090). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retained UI builds avoid storing compressed duplicates, keeping earlier tabs usable within the existing three-generation and 96 MiB limits. Earlier-build downloads use larger uncompressed files. [#163099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163099). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The community invitation links to Reddit, Discord and X and renews an old dismissal once. A new dismissal is retained when browser storage is available, and the opt-out setting is honored. [#163109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163109). Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The first browser connection after a restart avoids heavier startup-scheduling preparation. [#163173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163173). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Existing translations cover model connections, accounts and billing controls. [#163176](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163176).
- A failed Mention Inbox refresh retries after a minute instead of waiting for a distant expiry. [#163229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163229). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Enter on an invalid relative folder path preserves the input and listing while keeping that path unselectable. [#163284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163284). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Review stays unloaded when its tab is absent, while an existing hidden Review tab retains its content. [#163346](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163346). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Notification settings do less database preparation on the Gateway request thread, with saves completed before pending subscription changes proceed. [#163355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163355). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New credential fields accept the full masked value and receive focus. Previously redacted secrets and structured secret references remain protected and cannot be replaced in these forms. [#163487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163487). Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Paired Codex disconnection keeps guidance to start a fresh attempt. [Placement guidance](/plugins/codex-harness/placement). [#163515](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163515). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Tool cards distinguish unfinished work from reported success, failure or interruption, including recognized Wait and matching agent-completion events. An unknown outcome stays unknown. [#163545](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163545). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Reconnect bursts prioritize connection handshakes and spread retries, which can leave the first session lists taking longer to arrive. [#163714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163714). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The community invitation uses themed light and dark artwork and labeled Reddit, Discord and X controls with larger touch targets. [#163734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163734). Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
- The community invitation refreshes its copy in the existing translated locales. [#163738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163738). Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
- Unused vertical scrolling inside an inline widget passes through to chat. New video guidance can load a preview before Play, so it may download media in advance. [#163762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163762). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Concurrent conversation-history reads do less preparation on the Gateway request thread. Local terminal startup also avoids an authentication-reader maintenance race. [#163771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163771). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Add to chat comment editors stay readable while scrolling in desktop and mobile create or edit views. [Chat guide](/web/control-ui/chat). [#163772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163772). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- The Execution permissions heading is separate from Learn more, so clicking the heading keeps the menu open. [Permission modes](/gateway/permission-modes). [#163776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163776). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Tool Search activity cards show the tool actually called and its query, file or command instead of only the dispatcher. [#163829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163829). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session status and Workboard digests accept valid observer replies wrapped in plain or JSON code blocks, while rejecting malformed content. [#163878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163878). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resizing chats avoids redundant redraws while retaining scroll anchoring and hidden-pane geometry. [#163898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163898). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Malformed terminal attachments release their listener, slash commands retain their supplied help text, and MCP setup can continue when the browser has supported cryptography but lacks its UUID helper. [#164011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164011). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The terminal stays closed until overlapping shutdown waits have all ended, preventing partial or duplicate releases from reopening it early. [#164014](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164014). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avatar lookup stops on a temporary primary Gravatar error and tries a secondary address after a definite miss. Unsupported v2026.6.1 Control UI clients need a supported client because the retired configuration route is removed. [Migration guidance](/gateway/doctor/config-migrations). [#164022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164022). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- PR previews reuse unchanged checkout information while selected-ref, staged, configuration and ancestry changes remain visible on the next read. Unstaged-only counts can stay stale for five minutes, including after refresh. [#164035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164035). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An agent still preparing keeps a startup message instead of a settled failure, even after a minute. [#164041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164041). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Native Windows managed workspaces can be created or reused from deeply nested source repositories that hit Git's metadata-path-too-big error. Cross-drive metadata and separate deep-reference reconciliation limits remain. [#164249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164249). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- RGB-aware programs in local browser terminals use their intended colors when the host lacks COLORTERM. Explicit environment choices and catalog overrides retain precedence. [#164325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164325). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Large Claude CLI conversations use ordinary bounded pages and backscroll for local and native-only messages. Initial indexing still scans history, and an unavailable native transcript can still block loading despite readable local history. [#164419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164419). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed-work headings spell out elapsed time, with call totals on expansion and non-success outcomes visible while collapsed. Missing timing leaves Worked without an invented duration. [#164430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164430). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tool activity keeps a readable label such as Wait or Session Status when there is no distinct detail to show. [#164437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164437). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preference-only edits avoid an unnecessary Gateway restart when plugin defaults become available between reading and saving settings. [#164450](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164450). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@wenwenxiong](https://github.com/wenwenxiong) and [@SuperMarioYL](https://github.com/SuperMarioYL).
- Assignment and membership menus show all already-loaded matching people and agents in one scrollable list with search kept visible. [#164469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164469). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Normal agent preparation shows Starting up and automatically loads chat and sidebar data when ready. Preparation can take minutes; real failures keep their diagnostics. [#164532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164532). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Confirming composed characters keeps queued-message editing open and preserves the Side chat draft without sending. A later deliberate shortcut performs the intended save or send. [#164560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164560). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst).
- Eligible Activity and Current Work changes appear directly from session events, with smaller sidebar and dashboard list responses. Filtered or incomplete views still refresh, and a model change can leave some compact-row model details from the previous model. [#164573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164573). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Device-control text loads when Devices needs it, reducing the initial JavaScript download. [#164735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164735). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repaired container bundles let restricted arbitrary user IDs read the core Control UI assets, addressing the whole-UI 503 failure. Install or build the repaired image; existing deployments are not changed automatically, and private runtime files remain restricted. [#165937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165937). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@Conan-Scott](https://github.com/Conan-Scott).
- New Session ends a stalled model-catalog wait after a shared twenty-second deadline, explains that no session was created and retains the draft for retry. This deadline covers catalog waiting rather than the full workspace setup or first answer. [#165989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165989). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) and [@dprev](https://github.com/dprev).
- Chat updates return to on-demand scheduling after removal of the frame wait that could hold working indicators and draft updates. [d1dc3cd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d1dc3cd51104d5592968802de29b54002578117a). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Direct-linked automation editors and unsaved edits survive delayed initial agent information. Deliberate agent-filter changes and connection retirement keep their existing reset behavior. [a7d093b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a7d093b7e2d0c5e1d58e1e9c7185afbe49985a44). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed tools stop showing Running while the assistant continues. Without a definite result they show Outcome unknown; partial output alone keeps the tool running. [7b3972c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7b3972c677e20444376aeac1f3b2860633467b9d). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preparing the bundled web interface checks up to four retained files at once, verifying each before making it available. [4cf8c4d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4cf8c4d334a18a521f94314b0abbe357ff0bf470). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

</details>

### Updates and Maintenance

- **Update efficiency:** Package and source updates do less repeated checking and copying, keep the selected runtime and package manager, and retain recovery tools through replacement.
- **Installed updater limits:** Several fixes must be present in the installed updater that starts the operation. If an older updater refuses before staging, follow the [manual update procedure](/install/updating/update-methods#alternative-manual-npm-pnpm-or-bun) with a verified backup, then use the new CLI's Doctor and repair commands.
- **Windows 2026.9.4 automatic updates:** Affected automatic updates from Windows 2026.9.4 refuse an incompatible candidate; wait for the updater to exit, make a [verified backup](/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery#before-updating-create-a-verified-backup), and update manually from an independent shell using the same account, installation and profile.
- **Other Gateways and stopped services:** Stop other Gateways and installation-modifying tools yourself during updates; restart sibling Gateways separately. A stopped service stays stopped unless the updater owns its restoration.
- Saving compatible settings during update checks can now be followed by a fresh check instead of an immediate failure. The updater keeps caller and service profiles separate and still refuses incompatible or repeatedly changing settings. Rollback prepares restore copies before moving live databases and refuses restoration that would discard newer changes. Even legitimate Doctor migrations disable automatic restoration of changed databases, so recovery after a later failure may need manual action. See [rollback and recovery](/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery).
- Update reports stay visible through the final result, keep JSON output parseable and explain failures without exposing private details. An old failure can be labeled historical when the Gateway is ready and serving the intended target; its original result is retained. Eligible Linux update receipts can refresh after the filesystem's device number changes, allowing another update. The initial `update --dry-run` path can also rewrite those receipts, so that preview is not read-only.
- Doctor can repair supported session ownership and quarantined data, do less repeated archive work, and repair writable state with [read-only configuration](/cli/config). Required configuration edits still belong in the deployment source. Accepted settings and repair permissions remain protected, and explicit recovery references must verify before repair. If Doctor finishes while the managed Gateway is still starting, check `openclaw gateway status` afterward. Empty-transcript recovery can still break another agent's reference to a shared file; keep the originals and backups together at their reported paths. See [running Doctor](/cli/doctor/running).
- Very old configurations and saved-data formats may need an intermediate release before this upgrade can proceed; refusals preserve the old files. Back up complete configuration, state and queue-owned media first. The documented configuration forms need **2026.9.5 Doctor**; Matrix's five retired files also need Matrix started once, and older flat layouts first need **2026.6 Doctor and Gateway startup**. Retired outgoing/session JSON queues, cron files, encrypted OAuth sidecars, Voice Call logs and nested workspace setup files need **2026.9.7 on the original host, then Doctor**. Do not rename migrated queue artifacts to replay messages. Supported July routing aliases, supported JSON imports and current SQLite state remain supported. Follow [state migrations](/cli/doctor/state-migrations), [Matrix migrations](/channels/matrix-migration#retired-pre-july-state-files) and [incoming-queue migration](/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate#migrate-durable-ingress-files-through-doctor) for the affected format.
- Doctor gives eligible single-file [Talk configurations](/nodes/talk) their own saved provider settings, so later Voice Call edits do not change Talk. Configurations split across included files can lose inherited settings and need separate correction; see [Talk migration](/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#talk-realtime-inheritance). Supported command-approval policies also receive repair with a private original copy, but ordinary pre-Doctor exports can omit old command permissions or MCP grants. Run Doctor against the correct state directory before relying on an older-format export. Retired embedded session settings and shortcuts require backed-up manual correction; current Doctor does not repair them. Follow [session-setting migrations](/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#session-settings).
- Stopping the Gateway waits for accepted database maintenance and worker cleanup. A plugin cleanup timeout no longer turns a successful message command into a failure, though cleanup can remain pending. A final native-worker cleanup failure leaves that source unavailable until the process restarts. Restarts continue after duplicate configuration notifications, and crash-loop pauses keep interrupted-turn retries for replay after a full stable window. Running or queued work survives an access-policy change only while its original access remains valid. See [restart recovery](/gateway/restart-recovery#safety-valves-and-observability) and [message commands](/cli/message).
- Containers recognize a verified live owner of shared Gateway data even when process IDs differ between containers. A foreign or unverifiable owner needs a heartbeat older than 90 seconds before eligible recovery; Doctor refuses a fresh unknown owner. Use the documented shared process namespace for a separate Docker CLI container, or run the TUI inside the Gateway container. Windows recovery can re-enable a verified disabled Gateway task while refusing a foreign launcher. See [Windows guidance](/platforms/windows).
- **Archive cleanup and integrity checks:** Session and archive cleanup moves supported database work into background workers while keeping retention rules and denied-access checks. Automatic five-minute and daily full database integrity scans stop; requested quick checks cover less corruption than those full scans.
- **Source Control UI rebuilds:** When a source Control UI rebuild fails, the previous bundle is retained, but the wrapper still overrides custom `--outDir` with `dist/control-ui`.

Newer repair paths recover eligible abandoned updater children and readable retained conversation history. Windows pnpm users can repair an existing stale Gateway launcher with `openclaw gateway install --force` from the current installation; an ordinary reinstall may keep the registered task.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Idle browser nodes can update while upload files are retained, with restart recovery for interrupted cleanup. [#154384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154384). Thanks [@xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Doctor checks that an explicitly selected session store belongs to the requested agent; scripts with mismatched selectors must use its actual owner. [#156907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156907). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Older compacted sessions can enter enabled cold storage after the configured idle period while references to required history remain protected. [#157994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157994). Thanks [@anyech](https://github.com/anyech), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keeping a deleted agent’s history no longer blocks the affected plugin-store cleanup during Gateway shutdown. [#158473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158473). Thanks [@anyech](https://github.com/anyech), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Doctor accepts a service home override pointing to the account’s canonical home, restoring the existing credential-repair path. [#160056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160056). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@oscarsixsecllc](https://github.com/oscarsixsecllc), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- An already-stopped Gateway stays stopped when Doctor cannot verify its current ownership and offline state; inspect deep status before starting it manually. [#160083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160083). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can remove abandoned update files beside unrelated Node or Bun apps when readable process and package evidence confirms they do not use those files. [#160158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160158). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored).
- Failed-update recovery preserves databases changed during Doctor, including legitimate migrations; restoring those databases then requires manual recovery. [#160167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160167). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- A restart can skip repeated database scans after idle or finished writers close successfully, even when other shutdown cleanup reaches its deadline. [#160201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160201). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repair preserves settings for incompatible plugins and finishes pending saved-data confirmation after compatibility is corrected, including when packages are unchanged. [#160344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160344). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@EndeavorPioneer](https://github.com/EndeavorPioneer).
- Eligible database rollback retains current update attempts and failure details while leaving the original snapshots unchanged. [#160614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160614). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Updates refuse to replace a shared installation while an observed sibling Gateway is running; stop that service yourself and restart it after the update. [#160663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160663). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateways already running this fix retain cleanup code through package replacement and save final speech with heuristic notes; model notes can be regenerated after restart. [#160674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160674). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows Gateway inspection and update repair preserve empty arguments, quotes, backslashes and Unicode when identifying running processes. [#160680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160680). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and plugin maintenance can continue through brief competing database writes without immediately failing admission or losing maintenance ownership. [#160702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160702). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@Ludwigtheo0815](https://github.com/Ludwigtheo0815), [@laurentschwartz](https://github.com/laurentschwartz).
- Incompatible automatic updates from Windows 2026.9.4 stop before activation; wait for the updater to exit, create a verified backup and follow the manual update procedure. [#160718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160718). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Busy database writers move automatic checkpoint work away from saving changes, addressing one source of pauses on large stores; idle-worker checkpoint completion remains uncertain. [#160818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160818). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cloud workers retain their new runtime after a Gateway update until installation is recorded, avoiding a missing-runtime failure on the next turn. [#160820](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160820). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible scheduled session cleanup runs in the background and preserves publication records belonging to a recreated session. [#160858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160858). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New request errors distinguish a Gateway restart, shutdown or temporary pause and explain when to try again. [#160869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160869). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Running and queued work can survive live connection-policy changes when its original access remains valid; clients still reconnect, and actual access loss still cancels affected work. [#160909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160909). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A delayed node-connection alert is suppressed when its pairing check finishes after Gateway shutdown begins. [#160915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160915). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session-history cleanup can resume after its blocking writes are safely checkpointed even while unrelated writes continue; relevant open readers still postpone cleanup. [#161061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161061). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Interrupted update validation waits for accepted progress writes and retains temporary recovery data when their outcome is uncertain. [#161104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161104). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The current automatic source-server update script checks a private candidate before stopping the Gateway; preparation failures preserve the serving installation, while shared sibling services remain yours to stop. [#161110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161110). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Active session updates can commit while background maintenance performs its initial database check. [#161181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161181). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can recover interrupted imports of old MCP sign-in files without replaying retired credentials or undoing a later logout; uncertain outcomes retain recovery state and warn. [#161219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161219). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates and source rebuilds identify discovered sibling Gateways by their actual service name, including loaded Linux services with missing unit files, and explain which service to stop. [#161238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161238). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Source update completion and update repair refuse to replace files used by a discovered running sibling Gateway; stop overlapping services yourself and retry. [#161239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161239). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unmanaged foreground Gateways can remain alive for manual configuration repair after restart triage declines or fails; requests resume only after startup succeeds. [#161294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161294). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js).
- Older updaters no longer falsely report a changed database inventory and roll back because optional ownership details were omitted. [#161411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161411). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed update recovery skips waiting for a Gateway known to be absent, while retaining the original failure and restoration checks. [#161426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161426). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Source installations retain the previous Control UI bundle after a failed rebuild and can recover an interrupted replacement on the next build; the wrapper overrides custom --outDir with dist/control-ui. [#161438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161438). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor clears eligible installation-only upgrade warnings for disabled SMS and unconfigured Mistral without enabling the channel or adding settings; real migration work still needs completion. [#161535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161535). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@akennedytog](https://github.com/akennedytog), [@g62nkcx4q7-wq](https://github.com/g62nkcx4q7-wq).
- Docker package JSON receipts no longer leave an extra copy of the finished archive in the shared npm cache; initial packing can still use that cache. [#161548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161548). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shutdown and restart close shared native workers and their supervisor after accepted work finishes; cleanup failures remain visible. [#161558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161558). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor recognizes verified Skill Workshop history archives after their original workspace is retired, stopping repeated warnings while retaining original backups. [#161586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161586). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@percilabs](https://github.com/percilabs), [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng).
- Updates record and check the previous Gateway’s stop in background database workers while still waiting for durable confirmation before maintenance proceeds. [#161813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161813). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor archives strictly verified empty retired Telegram binding files so updates can continue, while preserving their original bytes and refusing meaningful or uncertain state. [#161832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161832). Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [@PennyVibe](https://github.com/PennyVibe).
- Managed updates record activation before stopping the Gateway and refuse a stop after ownership changes; accepted rollback can finish during interruption. [#161851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161851). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Doctor refuses to overwrite a recovery record after another owner replaces its maintenance files, retaining the existing recovery information. [#161861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161861). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update recovery loads fewer bundled components while keeping its existing service and recovery checks. [#161919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161919). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and local TLS Gateway checks work with a managed proxy enabled while retaining loopback routing policy and certificate fingerprint checks. [#161946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161946). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@saizero-dr](https://github.com/saizero-dr).
- Windows updates run by the fixed installed version restore the original task autostart state after cancellation following successful suspension and before installation changes. [#161990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161990). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automatic five-minute and daily full database scans stop; requested quick checks provide less coverage for index and page corruption. [#162015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162015). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor moves its initial contended Gateway ownership inspection into a background worker and checks cancellation and permission again before stopping a service. [#162021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162021). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows update cancellation can restore previously enabled autostart when disabling the task took effect before reporting failure, provided the original updater still owns recovery. [#162050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162050). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates started by the fixed installed updater retain SQLite verification workers after package replacement, avoiding the missing-worker startup failure. [#162076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162076). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint), [@paweldrozd](https://github.com/paweldrozd).
- Gateway shutdown cancels future background maintenance and waits for running callbacks before releasing resources; stopping one subsystem leaves unrelated schedules active. [#162079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162079). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates wait for saved progress before advancing, preserve authorized recovery after recording refusal and retain both operation and reporting errors. [#162098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162098). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shutdown retains native worker cleanup errors after the supervisor exits; terminal cleanup failure requires a process restart before that source can accept new work. [#162128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162128). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves existing scheduled-task history archives and saves newly imported legacy logs byte-for-byte under a numbered name, warning when archival needs retry. [#162152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162152). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Orderly shutdown waits for accepted database maintenance before closing storage; emergency process-exit cleanup remains best effort. [#162166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162166). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Direct binary upgrades with retired context budgets or the Copilot discovery switch must run openclaw doctor --fix before startup; managed updates apply these migrations before validating settings. [#162184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162184). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat-requested updates avoid repeated whole-database copies while Doctor checks current permissions; removing or replacing the requester still stops further changes. [#162200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162200). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DasX](https://github.com/DasX), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint).
- Doctor recovers supported old Telegram incoming queues without repeating imports or replaying historical failures; retired outgoing and session JSON queues use the separate 2026.9.7 repair route. [#162206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162206). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automatic session cleanup stands down during Gateway shutdown, avoiding repeated maintenance-worker startup; later writes can resume cleanup after restart. [#162212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162212). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor skips loading Codex recovery helpers when there is no legacy assignment state to inspect, reducing unnecessary work in empty scans. [#162215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162215). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows updates run by the fixed installed updater retry brief package-folder locks during backup for up to 57.75 seconds, retain warnings and explain persistent failures. [#162231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162231). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@miguelpt2026](https://github.com/miguelpt2026).
- Doctor reuses its update-history discovery snapshot to reduce repeated reads and offers optional phase timings for diagnosing slow repairs. [#162232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162232). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixed npm updaters exclude historical package backups from dependency scans and retire only eligible captured backups after verified activation; older running updaters still need the documented backup-preserving recovery. [#162246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162246). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Deleting an agent or running path-scoped maintenance now closes its session cleanup worker when the state directory is reached through a symlink. [#162248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162248). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update database checks retry transient changes when an inactive agent database becomes active, returning only a consistent snapshot; persistent changes and cleanup failures still stop inspection. [#162258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162258). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Successful update and repair progress is logged as information, advisories as warnings and failed phases as errors, while progress remains on stderr and JSON stdout stays clean. [#162270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162270). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Failed archival of verified empty retired Telegram bindings now warns and preserves the source for retry; uncertain or nonempty files still block migration with readable recovery guidance. [#162290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162290). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@PennyVibe](https://github.com/PennyVibe), [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star).
- Doctor repairs deep chains of session owners without a stack overflow, preserving the terminal session and conversation history without shortening the chain. [#162321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162321). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jasdeepohri-max](https://github.com/jasdeepohri-max).
- Failed updates with --no-restart retain recovery diagnostics through one inspection without waiting for unrequested startup, while package activation and required maintenance remain in effect. [#162327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162327). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@PennyVibe](https://github.com/PennyVibe).
- Doctor setting-conflict notices consistently name the full current-setting path when removing an obsolete alias whose replacement is already set. [#162336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162336). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Startup progress no longer hides actual errors from older updaters; reports retain useful redacted error details. [#162344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162344). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jamiezigelbaum](https://github.com/jamiezigelbaum).
- Updates started by the fixed installed updater avoid repeated whole-database copying and stalls from progress polling while retaining committed phases and the final report. [#162345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162345). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint). Thanks [@alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000) for the related pnpm terminal diagnosis and repair.
- Fixed installed updaters tolerate narrowly defined npm bookkeeping changes during package rollback and report bounded details about genuine mismatches; older running updaters retain their prior checks. [#162365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162365). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nessim-liamani](https://github.com/nessim-liamani).
- Database backups captured by a fixed installed updater retain their originally verified content facts so rollback can reject later changes before replacing live databases. [#162383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162383). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Source builds skip redundant plugin file checks and preserve tracked special filenames on supporting filesystems; add new auto-discovered packages and entries to the Git index before a root build. [#162384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162384). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- npm plugin updates reuse metadata already resolved for the same installation attempt, avoiding a duplicate registry lookup while retaining compatibility, integrity, policy and consent checks. [#162385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162385). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates using the repaired verifier warn about non-object plugin manifests and continue checking later plugins, while other configuration, ownership and readiness failures retain their existing handling. [#162386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162386). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Git update staging can use native directory copying when no disposable caches need filtering, avoiding unnecessary staging work while retaining real exclusions. [#162387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162387). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor uses less memory preparing large fleets with equivalent plugin sources, while preserving distinct plugin selections and workspaces. [#162394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162394). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
- Newer updaters can avoid recopying a private rehearsal database and preserve its original row IDs for migration checks; existing free-space requirements and older-updater limits remain. [#162403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162403). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Doctor prepares unchanged transcript archives before stopping a managed Gateway and reuses verified work on later same-version runs, reducing archive-dependent downtime while rechecking changed or restored sources. [#162404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162404). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@MikyWang](https://github.com/MikyWang), [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415).
- Doctor migrates covered sender tool policies to typed keys such as id:Alice while preserving the first effective policy; direct binary upgrades need Doctor before startup, and external-channel policies may require explicit migration. [#162427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162427). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update rehearsals avoid redundant backups of verified disposable core database copies while retaining ordinary recovery backups and separate configuration, plugin and Workshop captures. [#162429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162429). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Compatibility checks preserve current values when identifying older sandbox and embedded-agent settings; retired forms follow intermediate-version guidance. [#162430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162430). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor skips unused plugin completion checks when there is no deferred migration or verified retained legacy session source, avoiding an unnecessary detector pass while retaining ordinary repair checks. [#162486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162486). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Supported POSIX npm updates can recover interrupted package replacement with the selected Node or Bun executable, retaining the macOS SQLite library; recovery leaves the Gateway stopped for a separate restart. [#162586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162586). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor backs up and converts older pending-reply, fallback-notice and memory-flush state; sessions that still need conversion request a stopped Gateway and openclaw doctor --fix. [#162595](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162595). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates with matching recovery receipts can restore the previous package after SQLite moves already-captured data, while still refusing restoration that would discard newer changes; older updater receipt formats remain a limitation. [#162599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162599). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An installed updater containing this fix keeps progress recording from disrupting its own validation copy and reports progress errors without relabeling successful checks as failed. [#162604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162604). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves existing agent data and workspace ownership when converting older rosters; direct binary replacement requires repair before startup or ordinary configuration edits, with extra care for included configuration. [#162612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162612). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway shutdown keeps the database worker available to finish accepted memory writes, reducing repeated worker starts without discarding writes or shortening shutdown deadlines. [#162616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162616). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves previously accepted settings when a plugin repair fails or reports no changes, continues later repairs and retains warning-only guidance. [#162641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162641). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor migrates inherited Talk provider settings in eligible single-file configurations; include-backed configurations can lose those settings and need separate correction. [#162644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162644). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A present but broken channel repair module now stops Doctor with its original load error instead of silently selecting fallback repairs; genuinely absent modules remain optional. [#162645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162645). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Bun updates from the supported 2026.9.7 updater can finish candidate Doctor and activate the replacement Gateway instead of hanging; this repair arrives in the candidate package. [#162655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162655). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Once a stop or restart signal is accepted, the Gateway immediately refuses new work while continuing its existing shutdown and restart policy. [#162679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162679). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor repairs supported command-approval policies with a private original copy; ordinary exports before Doctor can omit legacy permissions and MCP grants. Run Doctor against the correct state directory first. [#162687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162687). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agent deletion and state-root cleanup through symlinks can release execution and history resources retained by archive pruning, preview and related storage maintenance. [#162700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162700). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Rollback prepares restore copies before moving current databases, so allocation or disk-space refusal leaves current databases and journals in place; later publication failures retain recovery files. [#162726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162726). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Doctor retires verified completed plugin-import obligations so later history can be imported, and update cleanup inventories recorded migration backups with guarded retirement after a later successful update; retain backups needed for rollback. [#162771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162771). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@shadow-enthusiast](https://github.com/shadow-enthusiast), [@islandpreneur007](https://github.com/islandpreneur007).
- macOS service diagnostics read the selected Gateway job’s own state and report unknown when launchd output cannot identify that job. [#162838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162838). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update timeout reports retain the check name, elapsed time and last sanitized progress; old installed updater deadlines still apply and observed progress does not prove completion. [#162883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162883). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nachoratico-source](https://github.com/nachoratico-source).
- Doctor can remove eligible abandoned updater runtimes after its inspection workers finish, identifies independent processes that still block cleanup and advises rerunning repair after their work ends. [#162886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162886). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed update reports put plain runtime-check guidance and the selected next step first, and label saved recovery observations so they are not mistaken for current Gateway health. [#162887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162887). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- When updates hand finalization to a fresh candidate after schema migration, transferred progress is confirmed in order before finalization; a refused write stops further work. [#162909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162909). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor recovers supported empty transcripts from verified backups or canonical history. With shared owners, recovery may move an empty original before every agent verifies it, leaving another agent's recovery record unable to find the file; keep originals and backups together at their reported paths. [#162946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162946). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@leoreq](https://github.com/leoreq).
- Cleanup can release a worker lease after first database creation even when read permission was revoked before its receipt arrived; the revoked operation still fails and leaves the session unchanged. [#162950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162950). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor repairs supported older credential fields independently of unsafe account renames, keeps usable current credentials and verified originals, and gives affected runtime readers the repair command. [#162958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162958). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An updater containing this change reduces repeated filesystem scans while preserving its runtime, with fallback progress inspection retained when copying stalls; older installed updaters do not gain this on their first attempt. [#162995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162995). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Update snapshots avoid redundant intermediate integrity scans while retaining source and final checks; the published 2026.9.7 updater’s initial snapshot still uses its older implementation. [#162996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162996). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Selected source builds avoid generating pure plugin assets twice while keeping manifest-writing hooks early and validating completed assets before staging. [#162997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162997). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Temporary update-validation Gateways skip restoring retained subagent runs, leaving recovery to the serving Gateway while retaining required configuration, database and readiness checks. [#162998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162998). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An updater containing this change verifies package contents with bounded concurrent reads while preserving fingerprints and fresh recovery checks; the benefit applies to verification work rather than a guaranteed whole-update speedup. [#162999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162999). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- On POSIX, an updater containing this fix settles its retained local database writer before capture, avoiding a false rollback refusal after Doctor errors while still protecting genuine newer data. [#163008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163008). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- More retired configuration forms now require the documented 2026.9.5 intermediate repair before upgrading, including old agent runtime, sandbox, Browser, queue, webchat and authored plugin-install formats. [#163015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163015). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Bun guide explains the installed 2026.9.7 updater’s temporary Node 24 workaround, keeping Gateway and updater on the same runtime and state, and distinguishing restored package files from completed Gateway recovery. [#163017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163017). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The 2026.9.7 Bun workaround now uses the ordinary published-release update by default; a particular version or local package remains optional, with the same readiness and state-preservation steps. [#163034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163034). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retired pre-July formats receive source-preserving refusals; supported July routing aliases and JSON imports remain separately supported. [#163036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163036). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A repaired updater retries eligible named npm or Bun dependency-cache failures once and preserves safe phase, error-code and package details in reports without exposing commands, credentials or private paths. [#163039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163039). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ttcmourao](https://github.com/ttcmourao), [@akrosliving](https://github.com/akrosliving).
- Archive discovery leaves very old global session files untouched; use a recorded or explicit path to inspect supported history. [#163055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163055). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves unsupported pre-July plugin install JSON and requires the documented 2026.9.5 repair with a compatible pre-update backup; supported SQLite upgrades refresh plugin discovery after schema repair. [#163075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163075). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Maintenance errors identify the operation that first lost access and distinguish storage failure from unavailable ownership. [#163103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163103). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Database heartbeat errors retain ownership-loss details already received before a worker fails. [#163134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163134). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Unconfigured old global session indexes no longer block startup; select the former store explicitly to import its supported history. [#163160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163160). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor restores supported July session-routing aliases while preserving backups; retired room-only grouping still needs an intermediate repair. [#163171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163171). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retired Telegram DM, thread and streaming settings now require Doctor in 2026.9.5 before upgrading; canonical nested streaming remains supported. [#163181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163181). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Historical encrypted OAuth sidecars stop upgrades without changing state; the documented repair uses 2026.9.7 on the original host, and residual-file recovery is not guaranteed. [#163186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163186). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Five old Talk realtime selectors need 2026.9.5 Doctor; refusal preserves settings and backups, including recoverable prefixed JSON. [#163189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163189). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cold-storage maintenance selects inactive archives in workers while protecting active sessions and retaining existing limits. [#163200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163200). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reload checks retire obsolete task-result and singular model fallbacks while preserving supported task envelopes and model settings. [#163207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163207). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Archive retention runs ordinary database cleanup in workers and rechecks changed references and files before deletion; retention rules stay the same. [#163218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163218). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor correctly names the installed plugin index in its existing manifest-drift warning. [#163220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163220). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Old cron jobs, split-state and run-history files require 2026.9.7 Doctor on the original host; supported SQLite jobs and quarantine imports remain. [#163221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163221). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Bun-driven Git updates use compatible Node from PATH consistently for candidate tooling while retaining the chosen Gateway runtime. [#163227](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163227). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- An obsolete memory-index layout now refuses upgrade without dropping its tables; recovery guidance calls for a July-or-newer backup. [#163271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163271). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retired agent and subagent model timeout fields require the 2026.9.5 repair step; tool-model timeouts remain supported. [#163280](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163280). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Source updates preserve the selected package-manager launcher when the qualified Node directory also contains pnpm. [#163310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163310). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat update notices show a short outcome and next step, with full diagnostics in Settings → Updates or openclaw update status. [#163318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163318). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Doctor continues authorized database repairs when an older updater cannot supply capture history, without inventing recovery copies or extending package rollback. [#163344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163344). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saving restart and update notices moves off the request thread while required saves remain enforced. [#163364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163364). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor archive maintenance avoids repeated full-database hashing and copying while checking current repair permissions. [#163369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163369). Thanks [@victor-gurbani](https://github.com/victor-gurbani), [@Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates repeat fewer package scans; completed runtime copies can remain when their layout prevents later cleanup discovery. [#163370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163370). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Git updates keep scoped pnpm ahead of competing copies even when the selected Node directory already leads or repeats on PATH. [#163380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163380). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Five retired Matrix state files require 2026.9.5 Doctor and starting Matrix once; older flat layouts first need 2026.6 Doctor and Gateway startup. Refused originals remain intact. [#163383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163383). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Later npm updates reuse settled file checks to shorten downtime; stop other tools that modify the installation while updating. [#163432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163432). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Optional orphan-result cleanup checks small idle batches after restart and provides fixed Git operation names for diagnostics. [#163445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163445). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restart recovery checks durable session stores in workers and rejects stale or cancelled results. [#163457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163457). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Routine package-backup cleanup is logged as progress instead of a misleading update error. [#163477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163477). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- npm upgrades retain installation identity through macOS directory aliases while the package directory is temporarily absent. [#163479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163479). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Startup Git discovery retries one timeout and reports unavailable facts; exhausted results stay cached, with Dev refresh or restart needed to recover. [#163500](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163500). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unrelated ancestor packages stop falsely blocking update checks, and two matching candidate-Doctor failures pause automatic retries until a new candidate or manual intervention. [#163541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163541). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@iJaack](https://github.com/iJaack).
- Legacy memory conversion processes vectors one row at a time, and updated updaters retain crash signals and bounded redacted diagnostics. [#163640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163640). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fenglanhua](https://github.com/fenglanhua).
- Git update and rollback reports show both recorded version numbers and short commits. [#163683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163683). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Experimental pre-schema-8 session and retired search layouts require complete-copy recovery through 2026.9.7 Doctor; released memory, auth and cache formats stay supported. [#163723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163723). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor guidance distinguishes supported provider-alias repair from the separate 2026.9.5 route for room-to-groupChannel state. [#163729](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163729). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible update recovery checks and completes pending shared and agent migrations before restarting; unresolved or newer schemas refuse with manual guidance. [#163803](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163803). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@chffhc](https://github.com/chffhc).
- Doctor recognizes verified Linux systemd environment overrides and still reports genuinely missing managed settings. [#163847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163847). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@travellingsoldier85](https://github.com/travellingsoldier85).
- Gateway logs identify the client requesting a valid chat or session stop, including fast requests previously missing attribution. [#163851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163851). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor restores Teams warnings directing retired JSON data through OpenClaw 2026.9.5 and doctor --fix before upgrading. [#163896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163896). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed updates retain their specific reason and original installer error on stderr in normal and JSON modes. [#163901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163901). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Verified session-import receipts survive macOS VM device-number changes; Doctor upgrades older receipts without replaying completed imports. [#163904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163904). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@paulotvlincelia](https://github.com/paulotvlincelia).
- Retired embedded session settings and shortcuts need backed-up manual correction. Setup utility-model selection, inline Pi API inheritance and prototype-named Talk voice aliases also receive fixes. [#163919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163919). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sandbox permission edits avoid unrelated service restarts; slow service cleanup stays isolated and required restarts retain bounded shutdown grace. [#163931](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163931). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Diagnostic export retires automatic conversion of pre-July trajectory entries while preserving source files and current SQLite export. [#163934](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163934). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Git backups restore triggers containing CASE expressions; retired same-file memory indexes require a preserved compatible copy through 2026.9.7. [#163945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163945). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Worktree cleanup and recovery use the owning Gateway; uncertain outcomes require inspection and sandbox recreation requires exclusive offline ownership. [#163953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163953). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Status marks a saved update failure as historical when the ready local Gateway serves the intended target, preserving the original failure record. [#163991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163991). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sergiorodriguez-sudo](https://github.com/sergiorodriguez-sudo).
