- Migration planning explains an invalid copied-state path before inspecting delivery queues. [#164007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164007). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Future npm updates using the fixed installed updater verify fresh package contents with bounded hashing workers and unchanged integrity checks. [#164052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164052). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updated nodes accept the Skill Workshop launch field used by a 2026.9.8 Gateway, preserving affected hosted turns. [#164068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164068). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Database opening and index repair avoid repeated metadata checks while retaining full integrity and foreign-key safeguards. [#164099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164099). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed-delivery health counts avoid unrelated history scans without planner statistics, with a one-time writable index rebuild. [#164131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164131). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor media repair removes redundant table scans and still refuses concurrent changes; stop competing database users before retrying. [#164139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164139). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Supported service recovery and local diagnostics remain usable with invalid configuration while startup validation and newer-version guards remain. [#164158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164158). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update reports retain recognized worker error causes while removing private paths and filename suffixes. [#164169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164169). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixed installed updaters avoid discarded duplicate configuration reads while retaining fresh edits and database compatibility checks. [#164182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164182). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin snapshots and runtime preparation overlap bounded metadata checks without changing discovery order or integrity checks. [#164183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164183). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Verified pnpm 11 and 12 isolated updates skip an unnecessary global-root subprocess while retaining fallback discovery. [#164184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164184). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Trusted official plugin prerelease updates reuse successful package-selection metadata within the same attempt, retaining integrity, consent and warnings. [#164185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164185). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Caller and service profiles using the exact same state path share one database backup per checkpoint, with fresh checks retained. [#164186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164186). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed-update advice correctly identifies a verified serving Gateway while preserving failed status and restart or rollback restrictions. [#164193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164193). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Doctor repairs writable state under externally managed read-only config and reports required source edits; older-image rollback may need a matched backup. [#164235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164235). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@JD8855122](https://github.com/JD8855122).
- Docker CLI containers preserve fresh Gateway ownership across process namespaces and explain how to share the namespace or run TUI inside the Gateway container. [#164236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164236). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@JakeMalis](https://github.com/JakeMalis).
- Completed Doctor repairs warn and exit successfully when the managed Gateway is still starting; check gateway status afterward to confirm readiness. [#164261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164261). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault).
- Update JSON output omits the human cleanup-progress line while retaining structured cleanup results. [#164312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164312). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates detect retired Voice Call logs before replacing the Gateway and direct affected users through 2026.9.7 Doctor first. [#164330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164330). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repair timeout JSON retains the failed result and original stuck phase even when Gateway restoration also fails. [#164337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164337). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can publish unchanged pre-migration backups when Linux creation-time metadata is unavailable, retaining file identity, size, hash and ownership checks. [#164363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164363). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@StevenKrebs](https://github.com/StevenKrebs), [@kirylh](https://github.com/kirylh), [@stunt4711](https://github.com/stunt4711).
- Nested workspace setup files require 2026.9.7 doctor --fix before further upgrades; retired nested bytes remain untouched and root-level imports remain supported. [#164399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164399). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixed updaters accept recreated command links with unchanged targets; older Claude Code recovery prompts warn that normal customization applies. [#164438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164438). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor finishes database cleanup before releasing maintenance resources, preventing its leftover leases from blocking a later repair. [#164503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164503). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Updates proceed around retained agent-deletion holds while Doctor preserves uncertain stores and explains reserved-agent recovery. [#164509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164509). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@rosmiroslav-create](https://github.com/rosmiroslav-create), [@PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13).
- Container ownership recovery uses proven process death or a heartbeat older than 90 seconds, protecting verified live owners. [#164546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164546). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Alex-vonAllmen](https://github.com/Alex-vonAllmen), [@coygeek](https://github.com/coygeek).
- Doctor reports the actual newer-database or recovery-manifest result; failed deletion of a disposable inspection snapshot warns after its reader closes. [#164697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164697). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and gateway start can re-enable a verified disabled Windows Gateway task, with profile-aware guidance and foreign-launcher refusal. [#164728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164728). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@taoxia255-del](https://github.com/taoxia255-del).
- Reports retain Doctor’s check-completion message after a later crash; a nonzero or signal exit still fails validation. [#164853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164853). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@cunzai007-hub](https://github.com/cunzai007-hub).
- Message commands keep their action result when a plugin shutdown hook times out, with unfinished shared-state cleanup reported as deferred. [#164915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164915). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates from 2026.10.1-beta.1 retain the cleanup imports needed after package replacement. [#165855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165855). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Doctor imports legacy pending replies from JSON sessions without repeating the repair refusal, preserving exact session IDs, transcripts and original archive bytes. [#165866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165866). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
- Crash-loop safe mode preserves interrupted-turn retries and resumes replay after the full stable window, unless another crash or shutdown intervenes. [#166238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166238). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@KoalaShen](https://github.com/KoalaShen).
- The new updater refreshes eligible completed Linux receipts after device-number changes; initial update dry-run can also rewrite those receipts. [#166394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166394). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@neilofneils404](https://github.com/neilofneils404).
- The 2026.10.1-beta.2 updater bridge inventory is restored and excludes standalone worker copies from bridge selection. [#167091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167091). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@adele-with-a-b](https://github.com/adele-with-a-b), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Candidate compatibility checks leave original profile databases and their SQLite sidecar files unchanged when preservation is requested. [5a19f360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5a19f3608c28baf0a890526c82bf4e09f5dde32b). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Candidate checks preserve original agent database files when requested and still refuse newer schemas saved only in the log. [89217d3b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/89217d3b875373f5ba4f63dfc5e23fa9ca744ba6). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Temporary database snapshots survive module reloads; shutdown waits for readers and stale cleanup cannot remove a replacement snapshot. [7b64dada](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7b64dadaeeb43212fb23787feaf8722f56a45032). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explicit channel changes keep caller and managed-service compatibility checks separate, preserving each profile’s settings. [1794d8b4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1794d8b4ef8dde46f39a16da2bdbcf0bf2b519ef). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates recheck settings saved during validation and reselect an implicit channel when needed; incompatible or repeatedly changing settings still stop activation. [b36eb3e7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b36eb3e7b11afb2774d1f6ab41977df4713269ff). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update progress saves run in database workers; delayed saves cannot let a replaced or revoked update continue. [ea4aaef1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ea4aaef16715c65bf9a9530a1e275bdbf84f548e). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Configuration-triggered Gateway restarts continue after duplicate file-watcher notifications while retaining readiness and secret checks. [a4cc77c3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a4cc77c355f50dc650087815b43a2b73a34c29ae). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update verification can continue when its starting directory disappears by launching the read-only worker from a valid temporary directory. [8e303782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8e303782bb5677edbb27c77c222b568fb692dbbf). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Corrected installed updaters preserve relative user, session and local-package paths while replacing an installation that contains their starting directory. [6830c82e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6830c82ea080767b8953edce13a3e543eed419da). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Interactive update progress remains visible through final verification and result reporting. [82fe5e96](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/82fe5e962f72a54bc80c43cb9b2a116228bafb3b). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible Windows updates settle pending database log writes before rollback capture; another connection still limits snapshots to manual recovery. [9863e3f9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9863e3f97ab4ad345ebbdcb575e6d310b13d40a1). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Pending session-maintenance callbacks stop when database access is refused, avoiding unhandled errors without changing the restriction. [5ca95288](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5ca95288927072852018ac363be57bb5154032a6). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway inspection recognizes Bun watch and hot commands and separate Node heap-limit values; worker diagnostics omit runtime stack fields. [4c7d05d9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4c7d05d917325b77836455a55837ecb2b1b23eef). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Blank migration-plan snapshot paths receive the specific argument error in both human and machine output. [71b857a7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/71b857a75b52b64bded377bda4f53086897a8283). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retired outgoing and session JSON queues require a complete state-and-media backup, original-host 2026.9.7 and its Doctor before retrying; retained recovery artifacts must not be replayed. [ff96d47c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ff96d47c3506c50123a555332e6a6cc576b2559f). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway startup leaves webhook config bytes unchanged for completion-only markers; actual repairs require Doctor and a subsequent restart. [b978fc0e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b978fc0e0e7107b1a6bfa00989557e0aa51d2641). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor backs up and repairs supported Claw ownership-history columns before planning; package removal retains current ownership and protects modified or shared files. [de1f4385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/de1f4385dc66f31f586b79f3e274007e0e3da253). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Overlapping session-recovery requests read after the preceding recovery is saved, avoiding false conflicts while retaining access checks. [2a206318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2a206318a7f53bfaa0aa9112c21aed405ac6ab16). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shareable failed-update reports retain a public-safe reason after private details are removed, including failures before any command ran. [fc7e71ba](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fc7e71bad25845ba8c23ddd6f3c44ded11a9f427). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@iancollyer87](https://github.com/iancollyer87) for reporting the problem.
- Doctor and temporary agent commands wait for their background database maintenance before releasing access or removing storage. [b58e6e98](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b58e6e98933e82d956cad7991c3df4c312665f8c). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Snapshot errors keep their original explanation when temporary-file cleanup also fails. [20f485c7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/20f485c7af9185eb29707fb7d8d879ce6b88ca33). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can inspect quarantined state to begin repair when no active or unverifiable Gateway owner blocks maintenance; ordinary reads still respect quarantine. [6d4ee0af](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6d4ee0afd92e9636b81b6e9bdb65a7367f072bc6). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Already-current updates can apply recognized legacy configuration repairs without a channel option, preserving the stored channel. [d1006ac0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d1006ac0a2066bfc78334b4a5433c1ded40a9051). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Candidate configuration checks retain supported legacy repair plans without switching channels or writing the original file. [107b2353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/107b2353d13e2a793ef1fbac6301585b310bccac). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Update rehearsals preserve historical webhook listener settings for Telegram, Feishu, Teams and Nextcloud Talk when a missing official plugin install is deferred. [237b7b91](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/237b7b91fd69e5d3963d9e94eb8f727768530726). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) for the original webhook-migration repair.
- Update, Doctor and delivery ownership timers handle backward clock changes and distant deadlines without expiring prematurely. [15405665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1540566502bde1656f525ef4f2b9283d1d310379). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Doctor can recover readable retained conversations when an older import record points to another database, preserving current settings and known deletions. Missing or ambiguous history can remain deferred; that warning alone no longer blocks updater-owned Gateway restoration. See [SQLite maintenance](/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance). [#167538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167538). Thanks [@bipolar84](https://github.com/bipolar84), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- With a fixed updater installed, `openclaw update repair` can clear a pending recovery record when its candidate package is already installed, retaining the recovery evidence and allowing later updates. Other generation mismatches still require inspection. [#167648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167648). Thanks [@cbhawthorne84](https://github.com/cbhawthorne84), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed-update rollback can restore the previous installation without waiting through the normal drain timeout when the Gateway is confirmed stopped and its port is free. A refused connection alone is insufficient. [#167649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167649). Thanks [@alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), [@hannnnn-l](https://github.com/hannnnn-l), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Triage reports pending package recovery as unrepaired and points to `openclaw update repair`, even when Doctor, current health and the last update record look successful. This guidance does not perform the repair. See [update recovery](/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#update-repair). [#167782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167782). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault).
- New Windows Gateway launchers for verified pnpm global installations follow package replacement instead of pointing to a removed version. Repair an existing stale launcher with `openclaw gateway install --force` from the current installation. See [Windows setup](/platforms/windows). [#167905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167905). Thanks [@SMF12138](https://github.com/SMF12138), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- `openclaw update repair --yes` can release eligible abandoned child ownership left by an earlier updater, including after a manual upgrade. It can also finish beside an ordinary active updater while leaving that updater's ownership intact. Live or uninspectable abandoned-work owners, surviving process groups, ambiguous history and unfinished rollback remain protected; the original OS account and inspection permissions may be needed. See [repair and recovery](/cli/update/repair-and-recovery). [#168043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168043), [#168197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168197), [#168198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168198). Thanks [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell), [@frankhli843](https://github.com/frankhli843), [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), [@hannnnn-l](https://github.com/hannnnn-l), [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson), [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@SMF12138](https://github.com/SMF12138), [@Tosko4](https://github.com/Tosko4), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@yu-xin-c](https://github.com/yu-xin-c).
- Doctor and update repair reuse already recorded continuation or takeover permissions after checking current ownership, avoiding redundant database scans. Cold reads and other service-inspection delays can still remain. See [Doctor recovery](/cli/doctor/recovery). [#168069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168069). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Tosko4](https://github.com/Tosko4).
- Post-install Doctor works when updating from older versions, including 2026.8.2, without an environment-variable workaround. Invalid run IDs and receipts belonging to another Doctor invocation still reject. [0322c196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0322c1969400d2674094f829d0a0a294ebd77947). Thanks [@GoDiao](https://github.com/GoDiao), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

### Messaging

- Telegram replies can use the related photos, forwarded notes and attachments together when they are already queued on a busy host or an eligible attachment download is slow. Albums keep their arrival order within each chat or topic, and successive messages from one sender can queue separately behind a busy agent. Collection remains bounded, so late arrivals can still form another turn.
- Telegram agents can see supported images sent as files when they have [image capability](/nodes/media-understanding). Hyphenated commands remain copyable, deeply nested replies become readable text with media links, and [rich messages](/channels/telegram/rich-messages) keep explicit numeric user mentions. Long single-agent tasks can deliver completed [progress messages](/channels/telegram/messaging) through reply plugins, respecting streaming opt-outs. Progress links remain clickable without website preview cards.
- Sending several files in a supported quoted list or compact array keeps every attachment in order, including filenames with spaces or commas. Long code blocks and unusual line endings keep their formatting and captions. Supported speech-only answers can complete alongside model reasoning without filler chat text. See the [TTS reply guide](/tools/tts/commands) and [restricted-profile setup](/tools/tts/api#tool-profiles).
- Replies from a remote worker attach its actual files, including completed replies sent before the workspace is copied back. Copy outputs from outside its assigned workspace into that workspace before attaching them; unreadable files produce a labeled failure instead of substituting a Gateway file.
- A queued follow-up can still finish after its original request closes, and a system-clock change no longer stretches its normal quiet period. Canceling while model settings load frees the conversation for your next message. Reset and deletion wait for channel work that has already started to settle.
- Background-command and restart replies return to the eligible web conversation they belong to. Affected channels keep answering after wake-from-sleep maintenance, and a channel with unchanged settings can finish a prepared reply after an unrelated plugin reload. Successful background media requests no longer show a false missing-reply notice while waiting for the result.
- Allowing a Discord parent channel lets the agent discover its active threads without a server-wide wildcard; explicit thread denies remain effective. Long model IDs retain the full selection through Submit; reopen `/models` if the catalog no longer resolves it. Voice joins and playback use elapsed time, so changing the system clock does not stretch or cut short that allowance.
- Slack fallback progress cards show quieter narration by default; the existing option still enables detailed tool activity. Slack and Matrix previews wait before displaying fragments that might be a silent-reply instruction. Matrix preserves unrelated contacts by stopping contact-map writes when account reads fail; retry the repair after reads recover.
- Each [WhatsApp named account](/channels/whatsapp) uses its own group reply preference, falling back to its configured mention policy. If a group relied on the old shared setting, rerun `/activation always` or `/activation mention` in that group with the intended account. Doctor preserves configured accounts and warns about ambiguous unlinked defaults; review them yourself and preserve needed shared policy before removing an unwanted default.
- IMAP catch-up batches messages, Tlon invitations can be renewed, malformed Mattermost callbacks receive an invalid-request response, and Twilio disconnects clear stale call-stream registrations. Early [Reef sends](/channels/reef) without delivery records can remain unknown; check with the recipient before resending. A late rejection of those old sends no longer starts the 15-minute cooldown, so a later rejection may allow one automatic rephrased resend.
- Upgrading very old channel settings or data can require a bridge release. Keep a **complete pre-update backup** and follow the [old-version upgrade guide](/install/updating#upgrading-very-old-versions). Retired Nextcloud Talk replay caches, Discord JSON preferences/bindings, Telegram sidecars and selected Matrix/Slack settings require the **2026.9.5** bridge. Retired Discord voice-TTS and guild-channel settings require **2026.9.7** Doctor. Migrate old Matrix JSON state before updating, and never run an older release against an already upgraded shared database. Very old Mattermost `allowPrivateNetwork` aliases lose their Doctor conversion; preserve the original configuration because a confirmed recovery path remains unresolved.
- If Telegram settings contain a full bot endpoint instead of an API root, run `openclaw doctor --fix` and restart; read-only sources need manual correction. After manual package replacement, affected older Telegram polling offsets and shared-root WhatsApp credentials also need Doctor before account startup. Old unversioned Telegram reply context is unavailable until messages are observed again, and assistant replies without recorded identity can appear twice in context. Supported conversation history remains available.

Telegram settings commands and skill invocations can wait behind a pending question without losing it. Stop, `/new` and `/reset` still cancel pending work.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete fix list</summary>

**Telegram albums and forwarded messages**

- Eligible forwarded attachments are buffered before download, keeping their note and context together during slow downloads; retryable download failures leave the burst pending. [#162787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162787), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram forwarded text and media can combine across separate polls within a one-second quiet window and the five-second batch deadline. [#163286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163286), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Photo albums queued behind a busy agent retain their chat or topic arrival order, avoiding retries that could replay or misplace photos while waiting. [#163926](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163926), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Same-sender Telegram messages and forward batches can take separate places in the queue while the agent is busy, avoiding timeout-related replays and accidental merging while waiting. [#164549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164549), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram holds album and forward collection for matching items already queued under local backlog. Collection remains bounded, so later arrivals can form another turn. [db966d6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/db966d62ae0fc6ec3da692dfd5dee3707e8a5b7a), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Telegram replies, rich messages and progress**

- Telegram routes supported image files to image-capable agents without requiring users to resend them as compressed photos; PDFs remain documents. [#141292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141292), thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@sercada](https://github.com/sercada).
- Hyphenated plugin commands remain complete and copyable in Telegram command help, including across list pages. [#141332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141332), thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- When Telegram cannot delete a replaced media message, it attempts to clear the old inline controls; controls can remain if the edit also fails. [#159012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159012), thanks to [@wakqasahmed](https://github.com/wakqasahmed), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- A guarded preview from a replaced Telegram turn cannot overwrite the current turn's message after a scheduling delay or flood wait. [#160137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160137), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Deeply nested Telegram rich replies render excess nesting as readable text with media source links instead of overflowing the call stack. [#160437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160437), thanks to [@Ayushdevo](https://github.com/Ayushdevo), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@hpyhandsome](https://github.com/hpyhandsome).
- Completed Telegram [streaming progress blocks](/concepts/streaming) can appear during a long single-agent task with reply plugins applied, while explicit streaming and block opt-outs remain effective. [#161546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161546), thanks to [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [@sercada](https://github.com/sercada).
- Invalid Telegram topic zero is rejected before sending; use a positive topic ID or omit the topic for an ordinary chat. [#162197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162197), thanks to [@adamczhang](https://github.com/adamczhang), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram rich messages turn safe numeric `tg://user?id=` links into named mentions when sending or editing. [#162523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162523), thanks to [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@alexph-dev](https://github.com/alexph-dev), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram progress links suppress website cards without changing the account's final-answer preview preference, including when progress and final text match. [#163727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163727), thanks to [@ivan-magda](https://github.com/ivan-magda).
- Telegram message-tool sends, including native inline buttons, work after affected ACP or chat handoffs without an invalid inferred reply target. [#165137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165137), thanks to [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).

**Attachments, text and voice replies**

- Completed subagent work sent through the direct-message fallback retains attachments, captionless media and voice-note intent when the requester cannot be awakened; a partial terminal send is not automatically repeated. [#142852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142852), thanks to [@Finn763](https://github.com/Finn763), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@hotfemale](https://github.com/hotfemale).
- Several whitespace-separated, fully quoted paths on one legacy `MEDIA:` line deliver as separate attachments in order, preserving spaces and punctuation in filenames. [#150958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150958), thanks to [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- TTS documentation shows `tools.alsoAllow: ["tts"]` for [restricted tool profiles](/gateway/config-tools/tool-policy#tool-profiles), with separate [automatic speech-output guidance](/tools/tts/output#auto-tts-behavior); restricted profiles do not gain TTS by default. [#155950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155950), thanks to [@fede-kamel](https://github.com/fede-kamel).
- Plain-text outbound replies preserve comparison text following affected emoji or astral Unicode letters instead of removing it as apparent markup. [#160850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160850), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Outgoing structured channel data preserves its own `__proto__` field when internal text is removed. [#160938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160938), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord and LINE preserve fenced code across long messages when newline-based streaming chunks are selected. [#162412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162412), thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Newline chunking with a zero or negative limit keeps a fenced block and its following paragraph together. [#163255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163255), thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Comma-separated, quoted `MEDIA:` paths deliver every attachment in order while retaining punctuation in names. [#163448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163448), thanks to [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313).
- Compact `MEDIA:` arrays retain every listed attachment and its order, including filenames containing spaces or commas. [#163627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163627), thanks to [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313).
- Replies using carriage-return-only or mixed line endings retain attachments, captions and literal fenced content. [#163665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163665), thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Newline chunking preserves Unicode line and paragraph separators inside fenced or inline code. [#163697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163697), thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
- A selected zero or negative media limit uses the existing bounded fallback instead of rejecting every buffer, restoring affected Matrix buffer sends. [#163701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163701), thanks to [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Remote-worker attachments use the remote bytes and enforce the assigned-workspace path boundary, including in completed live replies. [#164219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164219), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A nonblank paired `[[tts:text]]` speech block counts as a complete answer when the model also returns reasoning, allowing supported inbound channels to deliver audio without visible filler text. [#164285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164285), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Twilight-Networks](https://github.com/Twilight-Networks).

**Queued messages and reply delivery**

- Queued follow-ups resume after their normal quiet period when the system clock moves backward. [#160836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160836), thanks to [@nwang783](https://github.com/nwang783), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Eligible Control UI and WebChat background-command or restart replies return to their own conversation instead of the configured heartbeat destination; failed restart replies remain retryable. [#161006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161006), thanks to [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@oywino](https://github.com/oywino).
- Channel-reformatted replies retain the original conversation association needed for queued delivery and completion tracking. [#161123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161123), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Successful background media requests can acknowledge the request and later deliver the result without a false missing-reply or yield-failure notice while waiting. [#161154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161154), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Canceling a reply during model-settings discovery releases that reply so it does not block the next conversation message. [#161319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161319), thanks to [@Baumus](https://github.com/Baumus), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- [Custom channels](/plugins/sdk-channel-outbound) using the direct message drain with `deferredLaneOccupancy: release` can start the next same-lane message when the previous handler immediately defers. [#161476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161476), thanks to [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
- When a turn ends after its successful source-chat progress batch, OpenClaw avoids a redundant final-answer request. [#162367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162367), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- A sent final reply keeps its completion result and sent notification if notification for an earlier suppressed reply fails; the earlier error remains reportable. [#162509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162509), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Deliberately silent unauthorized group commands avoid false missing-delivery warnings. [#163148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163148), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Conversation delivery records preserve send order, and a saved reply remains consumed when its waiting request expires. [#163222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163222), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Affected channel listeners can continue sending replies after the maintenance work started on host wake has closed. [#163268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163268), thanks to [@scotthuang](https://github.com/scotthuang), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@ChenGuoShun1979](https://github.com/ChenGuoShun1979), [@Harvey-XuH](https://github.com/Harvey-XuH).
- Logs identify an unsupported action on a loaded channel instead of incorrectly reporting that its plugin is missing. [#163551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163551), thanks to [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@axeg0](https://github.com/AXEG0).
- A definite rejection before dispatch no longer produces a false uncertain-delivery notice on the next turn. [#163769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163769), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepared sends on channels without a preparation callback can finish after an unrelated plugin reload when their registration and settings remain unchanged. [#163893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163893), thanks to [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Completed approvals notify channel cleanup, including Telegram approval tracking. [#163969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163969), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Message-tool-only answers retain completion history while its database work runs in the background. [#164112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164112), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation reset or deletion waits for already-started channel processing to settle after queued cancellation; an idle canceled turn releases promptly. [#164247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164247), thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Chat retries report a retryable unavailable response when the original-message comparison cannot be read, instead of replaying an unverified answer. [#164313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164313), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Replies without a session key retain the session-ID and file fallback without attempting invalid keyed lookups or draining keyed events. [4a1c078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4a1c07851faa9aa7fb50bc8de4f08e3134949e88), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delayed queued follow-ups can complete after the triggering request closes instead of repeatedly failing against its closed work lifetime. [3400fd9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3400fd992cfce289f33e25271eaecfc7362e7e8d), thanks to [@xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen). Related proposal: [#148025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148025) (context).
- Incoming messages wait for session metadata to finish saving, avoiding a first-use database initialization race. [4324af4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4324af413b0606667368b063814f49a598db8af9), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retrying an affected canceled chat request returns its recorded timeout even after its reservation was lost; changed input using the same request ID still conflicts. [9b83d35](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9b83d350f1841d632f28fbb24153669e330a36be), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Discord threads, models and voice**

- Discord voice joins retain their connection allowance when the system clock changes. [#156979](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156979), thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Agents allowed to read a Discord parent channel can discover its permitted active threads and member records without a server-wide wildcard. Explicit thread denies still apply. [#160629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160629), thanks to [@PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@postoso](https://github.com/postoso).
- Discord attachment uploads retain the caller's mention restrictions when component delivery falls back to classic messages. [#161604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161604), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord and OpenAI realtime voice codec handling adds input validation and cleanup after allocation or partial-initialization failures. [#162592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162592), thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Discord playback deadlines tolerate system-clock changes, avoiding cutoff or delayed cleanup caused by that trigger. [#163652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163652), thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
- Discord's picker shortens display labels for model IDs over 100 characters while retaining the exact full selection through Submit. If the catalog no longer resolves the selection, reopen `/models`; shared prefixes can still produce identical displayed labels. [#164311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164311), thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Slack, Matrix, WhatsApp and other channels**

- The [Nextcloud Talk guide](/channels/nextcloud-talk) explains message receipt, permission checks and reply delivery separately, so a webhook acknowledgment is not mistaken for a completed answer. [#157715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157715), thanks to [@deepujain](https://github.com/deepujain), [@jm9151821](https://github.com/jm9151821).
- The optional [IMAP plugin](/automation/imap) fetches pending message bodies in batches of 20 during catch-up, preserving ordered retries without retaining every body at once. [#160413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160413), thanks to [@Ayushdevo](https://github.com/Ayushdevo), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@addyCooks](https://github.com/addyCooks).
- Slack and Matrix draft previews hold a possible lone-`N` silence-marker fragment while ordinary words beginning with N and a genuine final N remain deliverable. [#160582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160582), thanks to [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@imabotone-ui](https://github.com/imabotone-ui), [@nashtu3000](https://github.com/nashtu3000), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Automatic channel resolution accepts Slack workspace-qualified user IDs without the `--kind user` workaround. [#160619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160619), thanks to [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Slack fallback cards use quiet narration by default. Set `channels.slack.streaming.progress.toolProgress: true` for detailed activity; when explicitly disabling it while keeping fallback cards, also select `style: card`. [#161472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161472), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tlon can process a renewed group invitation after the preceding invitation ends, under its existing auto-accept and inviter rules. [#161596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161596), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A failed Matrix account-data read stops the direct-room mapping write instead of erasing unrelated contacts. [#161727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161727), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix silently declines excess requests when its pending pairing queue is full instead of sending an empty code or false approval reminder. [#161767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161767), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mattermost interaction callbacks containing JSON `null` return the invalid-body response instead of throwing a context exception. [#162603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162603), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- WhatsApp named accounts no longer inherit the old unscoped group activation setting; rerun the desired `/activation` command in the intended account's group when needed. [#162799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162799), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reef's model guard accepts the exact `gpt-6.1-sol` ID under its existing validation rules. [#162955](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162955), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Early Reef sends without canonical receipts retain unknown delivery status without imposing a historical-rejection cooldown. Check with the recipient before resending; later recorded rejections retain the guarded rephrasing rules. [#163205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163205), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tlon displays configured model names such as `constructor` and `toString` as ordinary strings. [#163438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163438), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Twilio clears even an empty stream-ID registration on disconnect so the next inbound call can stream. The ngrok loopback option requires valid Twilio signatures. Very old Mattermost `allowPrivateNetwork` aliases lose their Doctor conversion; keep the original configuration because a confirmed recovery path remains unresolved. [#163767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163767), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves existing channel account maps and WhatsApp shared policy. It warns about ambiguous unlinked defaults for operator review instead of deleting them; preserve needed shared policy before explicitly removing an unwanted default. [#163974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163974), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Matrix partial [message previews](/concepts/messages) hold punctuation-prefixed silence-marker fragments such as `.NO_REP`; divergent text resumes streaming and genuine final fragments remain deliverable. [#164259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164259), thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Channel setup and upgrades**

- Telegram troubleshooting distinguishes the Node networking threshold from OpenClaw's maintained [supported-runtime requirements](/install/node). [#159240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159240), thanks to [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
- Doctor imports implicit shared-root WhatsApp credentials into `credentials/whatsapp/default` with private `.migrated` backups and conflict preservation. After manual replacement, run `openclaw doctor --fix`, upgrading core first if needed. Explicit `authDir` stays authoritative. Remove the reported ignored `exposeErrorText` key, including false values, or use its documented 2026.9.5 Doctor bridge. [#163203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163203), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Nextcloud Talk preserves and refuses retired replay-deduplication JSON files; migrate affected state with 2026.9.5 Doctor in the same profile before upgrading. [#163216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163216), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix preserves and refuses active `thread-bindings.json` and `startup-verification.json` files. Migrate before updating and retain a compatible pre-update backup; an older release must not open an already upgraded shared database. [#163292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163292), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord preserves and refuses old model-picker-preference and thread-binding JSON files. Use a pre-update backup and the 2026.9.5 Doctor bridge before upgrading. [#163329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163329), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Zalo Personal Doctor repairs supported older direct-message keys for named agents without converting genuine groups; run `openclaw doctor --fix` for affected state. [#163399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163399), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Normal updates back up and repair older Telegram SQLite polling offsets before startup. Manual replacements need `openclaw doctor --fix`; sidecar-only historical state needs a complete pre-update backup and the 2026.9.5 bridge. Replace retired `groupMentionsOnly` with `groups["*"].requireMention` through the documented migration path. [#163418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163418), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord audits honor `allowFrom`, including an explicit empty override, and timed-out operations retain timeout errors. Doctor correctly names `dangerouslyAllowNameMatching`. Retired Discord voice-TTS provider and guild-channel `allow` or `agentId` settings require 2026.9.7 Doctor before upgrading; replacements use `voice.tts.providers`, channel `enabled` and top-level bindings. [#163728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163728), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix bounds pairing tracking and keeps original whitespace text when its compatibility chunker receives a nonpositive limit. Retired Matrix trusted-DM, flat private-network and room/group allow settings, plus Slack channel allow settings, require 2026.9.5 Doctor before upgrading. [#163740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163740), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Old unversioned Telegram reply context becomes unavailable until messages are observed again, and assistant replies without recorded identity can appear in both reply context and transcript. Supported history remains available. [#164173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164173), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram reports full bot-endpoint configuration and directs operators to Doctor repair before startup, probing or sending instead of silently trimming the URL. [#164414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164414), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Telegram settings commands and skill invocations wait behind a pending question instead of silently losing it. Authorized stop, `/new` and `/reset` still cancel pending work. [#168004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168004). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@vicary](https://github.com/vicary).

</details>

### Memory

- Search and Dreaming keep distinct memories in different languages, and Wiki searches retain accented and non-Latin words. Supported Brazilian Portuguese questions can prompt deeper lookup, and ordinary Conversation Summary notes become eligible for Dreaming. Blank searches offer keyword-retry guidance, filename searches avoid repeated scans, and recall instructions account for an optional Wiki. See the [session search guide](/concepts/session-search), [Active Memory lookup explanation](/concepts/active-memory/how-it-works) and [recall setup guide](/concepts/active-memory/enabling#remember-across-conversations).
- Long conversations can finish a summary that needs several stages when a plugin delegates to built-in compaction. Each request remains bounded; plugin authors must pass the original host cancellation signal for this fix to apply. Automatic summaries use your reasoning setting and current conversation size, keep requested AGENTS.md excerpts separate from unrelated material, and distinguish completed checks from work still needed. See the [context-engine guide](/plugins/architecture-internals/context-engines), [compaction settings](/gateway/config-agents/heartbeat-compaction-and-streaming), [compaction policy](/reference/session-management-compaction/compaction) and [overview](/concepts/compaction).
- Large conversation histories can become searchable again after database limits or a deadlock prevented indexing. Memory updates and cleanup put more database work in the background, and excluded system-only sessions stop falsely showing that the index needs updating. Forget keeps the original transcripts and excludes selected sessions from future indexing; if its result is uncertain, retry explicitly.
- Memory search can resume with your primary embedding provider after an outage, without restarting or rebuilding the index. A later search attempts recovery when the provider, model and settings match the saved index and the fallback is incompatible; attempts have a 30-second cooldown, and a working compatible fallback stays in use. Bedrock indexing also shares credential lookups without preventing rotation, while Zhipu batches split and retry when its 64-item limit is reported in Chinese.
- Memory search can again use an eligible saved Codex OAuth account for OpenAI embeddings without a separate API key. The account must have the required access: the separate Sign in with ChatGPT token-sharing grant does not authorize embeddings.
- Memory and skill edits use a 30-second polling default when native file watching is unavailable or polling is selected. `CHOKIDAR_INTERVAL` overrides that default, down to 20 ms; shorter intervals mean more background scanning. Allow for the interval plus scan and debounce time before an edit appears. Status explains the watching mode and fallback reason; restart OpenClaw after repairing the native backend to try native watching again.
- Very old Dreaming and Wiki installations need an upgrade recovery plan: Doctor no longer imports four pre-July Dreaming JSON journals or two older Wiki JSON formats. The files stay intact, and ordinary Markdown memories remain supported. Preserve a backup compatible with your current version before upgrading. A verified recovery route without a newer backup or established SQLite state remains missing; moving the old files aside does not migrate their data. See [memory guidance](/concepts/memory) and [upgrading very old versions](/install/updating#upgrading-very-old-versions).
- Check older Wiki settings and preserve retired Active Memory JSON preferences before upgrading. Doctor no longer repairs Wiki's `bridge.readMemoryCore` setting or warns about those preferences; an old Wiki configuration can fail validation without the former repair guidance.

Managed llama.cpp indexing uses smaller missing defaults while preserving explicit tuning. This lowers default concurrency and long-input capacity, so unusually long requests may need larger existing [llama.cpp limits](/plugins/llama-cpp). Memory Wiki can also reuse a completed CLI compile, and memory indexing and dreaming continue through provider reloads.
