**Search and recall**

- Ask for a keyword retry when a conversation search is blank [#129120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129120). Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@brainatworkharris](https://github.com/brainatworkharris).
- Recognize supported Brazilian Portuguese retrospective questions for deeper Active Memory lookup [#135137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/135137). Thanks [@wangyan2026](https://github.com/wangyan2026), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@wave-workflow](https://github.com/wave-workflow).
- Avoid repeated full-index scans for filename and path searches [#152585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152585). Thanks [@sahilsatralkar](https://github.com/sahilsatralkar), [@personaltrainerkoichan](https://github.com/personaltrainerkoichan), [@aron-intframe](https://github.com/aron-intframe) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Guide the assistant to use successful configured recall without treating an absent optional Wiki as a failure [#157089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157089). Thanks [@LLagoon3](https://github.com/LLagoon3) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keep accented and non-Latin words in Memory Wiki multi-word searches [#159683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159683). Thanks [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Clarify when configured recall runs and how to enable remembering across conversations [#161179](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161179). Thanks [@yashas-13](https://github.com/yashas-13), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@Raven-Gt](https://github.com/Raven-Gt).
- Make ordinary Conversation Summary notes eligible for Dreaming while filtering raw conversation turns [#161363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161363). Thanks [@Irish-Joseph](https://github.com/Irish-Joseph), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@Gabrielnkl](https://github.com/Gabrielnkl).
- Resume search after the configured embedding provider recovers from an outage [#163936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163936). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@cknzraposo](https://github.com/cknzraposo) and [@hubofvalley](https://github.com/hubofvalley).
- Preserve distinct multilingual memories in search and Dreaming selection [#164062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164062). Thanks [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415) and [@jarimustonen](https://github.com/jarimustonen) for the fix and acknowledged prior work.

**Conversation compaction**

- Estimate active conversation context without recounting history already replaced by a summary [#150623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150623). Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@BillBOT33](https://github.com/BillBOT33) and [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
- Honor the configured compaction reasoning level during automatic summaries [#159476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159476). Thanks [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@rtfcv](https://github.com/rtfcv) and [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
- Improve summary instructions to retain completed checks that found a problem and separate remaining remediation [#161293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161293). Thanks [@itayzit](https://github.com/itayzit) and [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Keep requested AGENTS.md sections separate from unrelated appendices while preserving fenced examples [#162735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162735). Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco).
- Restore transcript-based context estimates in affected non-Codex Bun conversation paths and missing Cloudflare plugin identity and activity artwork [#163051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163051). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Refresh the timeout between built-in summarization stages reached through delegating plugins [#163636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163636). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Indexing and maintenance**

- Run saved conditional instructions' database work in a background worker [#161395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161395). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse watcher paths to avoid redundant work when processing file changes [#161495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161495). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move memory maintenance planning and forgotten-session reads into workers [#161741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161741). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save generated search-vector cache entries in a worker while checking that the index is still current [#161831](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161831). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Count and prune cached embeddings in a worker, yielding between batches [#161884](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161884). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Finish Forget checks and writes in a worker, retaining previews and requiring explicit recovery after an uncertain result [#161959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161959). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move Forget preview and apply index scans into the retrieval worker [#162045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162045). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Introduce a 30-second polling default and expose fallback health; a later fix restores explicit shorter overrides [#162271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162271). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read source hashes and reusable cached embeddings off the Gateway thread during indexing [#162528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162528). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move durable-session memory accounting and side-chat history preparation into workers, stopping delayed accounting from starting maintenance after access expires [#163045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163045). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scan session-memory startup statistics in the indexing worker [#163194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163194). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Discover stored conversations for indexing, archived search and selective forgetting in the history worker [#163234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163234). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plan durable recall and Dreaming cleanup in the history worker [#163269](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163269). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Skip unnecessary embedding-cache eviction scans and remove only the oldest excess entries [#164083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164083). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share Bedrock instance-role credential lookup during indexing while allowing later credential rotation [#161164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161164). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@slinkoff](https://github.com/slinkoff).
- Stop excluded system-only sessions from falsely marking the memory index dirty [#161834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161834). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@alfred429](https://github.com/alfred429).
- Keep unresolved-session Forget dry runs from creating a missing memory database [#162155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162155). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore memory catch-up, updates and cleanup for large session selections [#164336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164336). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309) and [@nkarkare](https://github.com/nkarkare).
- Fix the rollback-journal indexing deadlock that kept affected large sessions out of search [#164624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164624). Thanks [@triciah73](https://github.com/triciah73) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Split and retry oversized Zhipu embedding requests when its 64-item limit is returned in Chinese [00c09b7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/00c09b79fc0f440c390e67c5540d0099137fb0ba). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus). Thanks [@vanecook](https://github.com/vanecook) for the report and exact error payload.
- Select eligible stored Codex OAuth credentials for OpenAI memory embeddings [f6883b3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f6883b3771c0a40d7bd62fcb99815dcbbb2e24f3). Thanks [@astra-openclaw](https://github.com/astra-openclaw) and [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

**Older-installation upgrade changes**

- Retire automatic imports of `memory/.dreams/daily-ingestion.json`, `session-ingestion.json`, `short-term-recall.json` and `phase-signals.json` while leaving their bytes intact [#163334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163334). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retire automatic imports of `.openclaw-wiki/source-sync.json` and `.openclaw-wiki/import-runs/<runId>.json` while preserving current SQLite rollback support [#163391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163391). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Remove the old Wiki `bridge.readMemoryCore` to `readMemoryArtifacts` repair and retired `plugins/active-memory/session-toggles.json` warning, without replacement recovery guidance [#163667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163667). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Automatic memory-saving turns keep their designated memory tools instead of regaining tools that can edit project files or do unrelated work. See [memory saving before compaction](/plugins/sdk-overview/memory-and-context#pre-compaction-memory-flush). [#166650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166650). Thanks [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Built-in conversations with smaller context windows can continue after automatic compaction. An individually oversized message receives guidance to shorten it or choose a larger-context model, while saved input and history remain available. See [compaction](/concepts/compaction). [#167900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167900). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory Wiki can use the current cache after a separate CLI compile instead of compiling an unchanged vault again. Changed imports, missing indexes or an invalid replacement cache still need compilation. [#167929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167929). Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Managed llama.cpp memory indexing adds smaller missing defaults: one slot and a 2,048-token context, with room reserved inside the input budget. Explicit native tuning remains authoritative. Lower concurrency and long-input capacity can reduce throughput or require larger existing limits for unusually long requests. See [llama.cpp settings](/plugins/llama-cpp). [#168020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168020). Thanks [@Deregtx](https://github.com/Deregtx), [@frankhli843](https://github.com/frankhli843), [@jj5638521](https://github.com/jj5638521), [@Nehanth](https://github.com/Nehanth), [@spunzon](https://github.com/spunzon), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory indexing and scheduled dreaming continue through Memory Core or provider reloads. Replacing an instance waits for a pending dream diary entry to finish, and retired callbacks cannot use the replacement. [eaf60b0e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/eaf60b0eb517b15c71438c9fd7c4e44154938f59). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Undoing a ChatGPT import in Memory Wiki refuses a folder at an imported-page path instead of moving it into recovery as a page. Missing files retain their existing handled no-op behavior. [08a1d712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/08a1d712b04b00e5244b506f2aa3201caa20e6c7). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

### Skills

- You can check whether a skill was found, meets its prerequisites, and is actually visible to the agent using [skill troubleshooting](/cli/skills#commands). After the next agent turn, [`/context list`](/concepts/context) shows the catalog that reached the prompt. Browsing and choosing from your personal skill library also moves database queries into the background.
- Installing an invalid skill no longer replaces a working copy, even with a forced local or Git install. [Sandbox skill copies](/gateway/sandboxing/workspace-access) can refresh from read-only installations on POSIX systems, with affected copies repaired on the next full sync. SDK sessions also recover nested skills mistakenly hidden by root-only ignore rules.
- Skills remain available after file notifications that report no content change, and development sessions avoid the resulting false restarts. Scanning skips temporary activity while still refreshing supporting files and rediscovering skills when needed. Skills and Memory now honor existing `CHOKIDAR_INTERVAL` overrides: the default is 30 seconds, the minimum is 20 ms, and shorter settings increase background scanning.
- Older skills and hooks need a manual metadata update to retain prerequisites, installation hints and hook events: rename `metadata.clawdbot` to `metadata.openclaw`, preserving existing canonical values if both blocks are present. Reconcile `.clawdhub` tracking into `.clawhub` without overwriting records. Where node clients are equally matched, select an exact node ID; the April 2026 macOS setup PATH-check exemption is also retired.
- Skill instructions give clearer routes for finding integrations: general requests use existing ClawHub plugins, while explicit skill requests use skills; installing either still needs separate authorization. Feishu Wiki instructions use supported browsing or a known wiki token. Callers must adopt the corrected tmux completion-marker example to distinguish fresh output from echoed commands and older results.
- Telegram skill shortcuts no longer take over the built-in `/export_session` command; a colliding skill gets a numbered alias that affected users should use instead. Multiline arguments beginning on the command line keep their interior indentation, tabs and blank paragraphs as received from the channel.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- After the next agent turn, `/context list` and verbose diagnostics report the skill catalog actually included in the prompt. Counts now reflect omitted catalogs and prompt overrides. [#145775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145775). Thanks [@tianhaotian](https://github.com/tianhaotian), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@adembektas](https://github.com/adembektas), [@aron-intframe](https://github.com/aron-intframe) and [@bulingbuling688](https://github.com/bulingbuling688).
- Troubleshooting documentation distinguishes a discovered skill from one whose prerequisites are met or whose instructions are visible to the agent. [#146951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146951). Thanks [@Frosmans](https://github.com/Frosmans).
- Personal skill-library browsing, revision lookup and selection preparation load their records without doing database work on the main Gateway thread. New selections check current permissions and exact revisions, while existing sessions retain access to their pinned revisions. [#164165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164165). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Local and Git installs fail clearly on unusable or oversized skill roots before copying files. An invalid forced replacement leaves the existing valid skill in place. Scripts that previously accepted an unusable source now receive failure status 1. [#160492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160492). Thanks [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@suninweb](https://github.com/suninweb) and [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Refresh cleanup removes stale sandbox skill copies while protecting mount targets and symlink targets outside the managed copy. [#162295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162295). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- On POSIX systems, sandbox skill copies refresh from read-only installations and existing copies repair on the next full sync. Source permissions, executable files and the guest's configured read-only access are preserved. [#162304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162304). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- SDK resource matching treats a root-only `/build` exclusion as root-only, allowing skills under a nested path such as `deploy/build` to remain discoverable. Anchored exceptions retain the same root scope. [#162455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162455). Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Skill scanning skips more irrelevant and temporary files while preserving file classifications between partial scans. If the classification cache fills, the next scan rediscovers files. [#161881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161881). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unchanged watched content avoids false development restarts, and Claude catalog watchers let one-shot commands exit. Skills and Memory polling honor `CHOKIDAR_INTERVAL`, with a 30-second default and 20 ms minimum. [fcdd870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fcdd87021745fd8352e362626b29b7a2e3a2c87d). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Legacy `metadata.clawdbot` is no longer read in skill and hook files. Migrate the needed fields to `metadata.openclaw` manually to retain prerequisites, installation hints and hook events. [#163104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163104). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Legacy `.clawdhub` records are no longer read and need manual reconciliation into `.clawhub`, preserving existing canonical records. Equally matched nodes now require an exact node ID instead of the old-client tie-break, and the April 2026 macOS PATH-audit exemption is removed. [#163965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163965). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- ClawHub guidance searches existing plugins for general integrations and skills for explicit skill requests. Installation still requires separate authorization. [#163848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163848). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Feishu wiki guidance uses node listing for a known space or parent and direct lookup for a wiki token, avoiding the unavailable search action. [#161755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161755). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The tmux skill's completion-marker example uses a fresh randomized marker split across `printf` arguments, so an echoed command or old output does not look like a completed task. Callers need to adopt the updated example. [#146329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146329). Thanks [@GodBlf](https://github.com/GodBlf), [@mrzeepek](https://github.com/mrzeepek) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram keeps the built-in `/export_session` command. A colliding skill receives `/export_session_2` or another unique suffix, so its command alias changes. See [slash-command guidance](/tools/slash-commands). [#163149](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163149). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Multiline skill-command payloads preserve interior indentation, tabs and blank paragraphs when text begins on the command line. Whitespace already transformed by a channel remains subject to that channel's handling. [#164151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164151). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>

### Native Apps

- Mac chat stays open while its Gateway finishes setup or reconnects, and opening a completed conversation can restore a failed Dashboard. Refreshing an unrelated profile preserves sign-in for the same connection. Switching the primary Gateway clears the old chat and preview cache, and dismissing an older notification no longer hides its replacement.
- You can sign in to eligible Cloudflare Access apps inside the Mac Dashboard and keep those app sessions across restarts. Use an updated Mac app with an observed HTTPS app on port 443 on the same site, Access team and account. WebKit restrictions can still require the embedded app's own sign-in link.
- Saved remote Mac Gateways can reconnect, open and switch after a valid certificate renewal for the same host and port that macOS trusts, including administrator-installed roots. An explicit `gateway.remote.tlsFingerprint` stays fixed and needs a deliberate update; expired, untrusted and wrong-host replacements remain rejected.
- Canceled Mac and iOS recordings stay canceled when microphone permission arrives late, and Android dictation keeps listening after an older attempt ends. Mac push-to-talk finishes on right-Option release, sometimes waiting briefly for final speech results; cancellation sends nothing. Voice fixes also preserve iOS stereo audio, repeated Voice Wake commands and opening words in Apple realtime playback through ordinary bursts and brief microphone delays. iOS Talk keeps voice directives out of later speech segments, and retired health checks stop reporting stale failures.
- Switching Apple conversations no longer lets a late summary, fork, rewind or settings save overwrite the chat you're viewing. Mac attachments stay with the chat where you selected or dropped them, case-distinct conversation IDs stay separate, and link previews match the current URL. Local database cleanup avoids a double-close failure, and Watch replies avoid an unnecessary retry pause after reconnecting.
- Android group edits stay with their original Gateway: switching connections rejects stale rename and delete dialogs, and deleting a group leaves its conversations Ungrouped. iPhone and iPad demo captures hide real saved Gateway and credential controls. Android keeps current notification forwarding while removing older recent-app suggestions.
- Update pre-July-2026 Gateways before relying on retired native-app connection fallbacks. Apple and Android retries with a saved device token now require explicit Gateway permission, and Android stops accepting older incoming field aliases. Apple apps also stop importing selected settings and certificate trust from before July 1, 2026, so older-only setups may need to reconnect and establish trust again. iOS and its share extension now use `openclaw-ios`; old client identity overrides and app-local Talk keys are retired, with local Talk using Gateway-configured keys.
- Reading and searching skills on Android has translated labels in 21 existing non-English locales.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Opening a completed conversation restores a failed Mac Dashboard; queued native actions can recover after a canceled replacement load. [#160886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160886). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A replacement Mac notification stays visible when an older dismissal animation finishes. [#161250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161250). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved Mac Dashboard sign-in survives unrelated profile refreshes on the same active connection. [#161464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161464). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Eligible Cloudflare Access dashboards support in-app sign-in and persistent app-specific browser sessions. [#161474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161474). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac menu-bar conversation and preview caches follow the primary Gateway, clearing former data on a switch. [#163892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163892). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac chat stays open while its unchanged Gateway completes setup or reconnects, including command-line chat launches. [#163971](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163971). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac learned certificate pins accept hostname-valid renewal trusted by macOS, including saved-Gateway opening and switching; explicit fingerprints remain fixed. [#164435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164435). Thanks to [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@joryirving](https://github.com/joryirving).
- Mac push-to-talk retires the correct hold on key release, permission delay or voice-mode replacement. [#143064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143064). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- iOS Voice Wake preserves both channels of queued stereo microphone audio when the capture buffer is reused. [#161148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161148). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restarted Android dictation keeps listening when an older permission or recognition attempt is canceled late. [#161391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161391). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iOS Talk removes directives from cumulative text, repeated Voice Wake commands work after listening restarts, and retired health checks cannot publish stale failures. [#161639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161639). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Late Apple compact, fork and rewind results cannot replace newer conversation errors or history. [#161799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161799). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Apple local-state storage avoids duplicate closing after rejected initialization and concurrent database misuse. [#162782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162782). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Apple transcripts keep case-distinct Matrix, Signal and catalog conversation keys separate while retaining structural aliases. [#162818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162818). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Apple realtime voice playback preserves opening words and ordinary bursts through brief microphone delays. [#163146](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163146). Thanks to [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez). Thanks [@Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman) for coauthoring the landed change.
- Apple requires explicit stored-token retry permission, and late microphone permission cannot revive a canceled or replaced voice note. [#163433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163433). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A failed Apple settings save from a departed thread cannot replace the current thread’s visible errors while its queued messages are put on hold. [#164037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164037). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delayed Mac file selections and drops stay bound to their original conversation, and Apple link previews show only their current URL’s title and image. [669db5d36e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/669db5d36e0637ba4c3033028ef050303963715b). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Watch reply recovery starts promptly when reconnect cancels an old history request, avoiding its unnecessary retry pause. [287a4003d3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/287a4003d34f14a3e3422456944bd6e1e6edb8ed). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iPhone and iPad demo captures hide saved Gateway and credential controls and remove contradictory pairing prompts. [#160857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160857). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iOS and its share extension retire old client identities and overrides. Local Talk uses Gateway-configured keys; Gateway-routed credentials stay on the Gateway. [#163339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163339). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android honors explicit stored-token retry permission and denial instead of the retired code-only fallback. [#163347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163347). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android in-flight group rename and delete remain on their original Gateway and reject stale responses. [#163420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163420). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android inbound events use canonical `payload` and `paramsJSON` fields; retired inbound aliases require dependent Gateways to update. [#163441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163441). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android dismisses stale or restored group dialogs on Gateway changes. Deleting a group leaves its conversations Ungrouped. [#163455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163455). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android stops importing the older recent-notification-app suggestion setting while preserving the current forwarding setting. [#163675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163675). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Existing Android skill reading and search labels gain translations. [#162602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162602).
- Apple retires selected pre-July settings and certificate imports; older-only setups may need Gateway reconnection and renewed trust. [#163002](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163002). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- When the Mac app waits for an existing background Gateway to start, it checks that the process is still the same before extending that wait. An earlier response cannot keep it waiting on a replacement process. [ad93d1ac](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ad93d1ac05c1cac3317d237637e25f656c244663). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

</details>

### Models and Providers

- Choosing a provider now keeps your model on that provider when an alias collides with its name. `openai/gpt-4o-mini` selects OpenAI; use `openrouter/openai/gpt-4o-mini` to retain the former OpenRouter alias route. Check affected selections, because the route can change credentials, availability and cost. Adding fallbacks keeps the primary model, queued choices survive older cleanup, and shared fallback changes require omitting `--agent`.
- Model status uses your saved nickname, and local terminal chat shows the model actually attempted after a fallback. Embedding output gives an eight-value preview, with full vectors available through `--json`; migration links lead to the relevant model guidance.
- [Bedrock](/providers/bedrock) can use externally rotated AWS profile credentials without a restart and continue after an earlier malformed tool argument. Setup preserves your chosen configuration when attempts are canceled, avoids unused connections, and reports self-hosted credential-storage failures before claiming success. Queue overflow also keeps protected entries while removing eligible ones.
- Windows local-model setup can recover when llama.cpp is missing Visual C++ runtime files by placing them beside the managed server. If that recovery fails, rerun setup or configure a compatible server manually.
- Older ZAI and Ollama Cloud configurations may need manual changes. ZAI usage checks now use supported configuration, the auth store, `ZAI_API_KEY` or `Z_AI_API_KEY`. Replace Ollama Cloud's retired `https://ai.ollama.com` address with `https://ollama.com` in `models.providers.ollama-cloud.baseUrl`; Doctor no longer corrects or warns about the old address.
- Official OpenRouter, Vercel and Perplexity requests identify OpenClaw to the provider through app headers. OpenRouter still receives that identification when install telemetry is disabled, and its Anthropic Messages requests replace configured Referer and Title values. Custom proxy hosts do not receive automatic attribution.
- Image generation with OAuth can retry using a supported OpenAI chat model in `agents.defaults.model` if the account rejects the default. Runway and Together video requests also honor longer timeouts. For ElevenLabs speech, manually replace the old `model` setting with `modelId` to select the intended model; see the [speech configuration guide](/tools/tts/configuration) and [field reference](/tools/tts/field-reference#param-model).
- OpenAI and Azure Responses keep your requested JSON output format, and recovered DeepSeek calls keep literal names and empty-string arguments. Models that see images directly avoid unrelated media-provider loading errors.
- Astra preserves completed answers after silent tool results without sending them twice. Custom OpenAI-compatible routes can opt into [stale-output pruning](/concepts/session-pruning). [Image guidance](/nodes/media-understanding) explains replay costs and replacing images with descriptions, which removes access to their pixels for that turn. Kimi K3's Code Mode fix renames the result-waiting argument to `awaitResults` on `exec`, `shellexec` and `agents_wait`; callers must replace `required`, which is now ignored.
- Failed requests give clearer retry hints and preserve the original rejection, while local SQLite errors stop pointless model switching. Eligible malformed or output-limited tool calls get at most one recovery attempt; incomplete calls do not execute, and completed results stay available. Check results before repeating work, because earlier actions may already have finished. Remote Codex launch failures point to execution approval without rotating healthy credentials.
- A solo Codex conversation can delegate even when native admission hooks are unavailable; another signed-in person's message is queued as a follow-up. Conversations affected by the earlier source-main regression need a new conversation to recover. A stale readiness check gets one refresh before a child follow-up is refused.
- Switching or replacing a Codex chat protects the new chat from delayed commands and cleanup. Child assignments survive parent-thread replacement on the same connection, and an interrupted chat can resume after stopping and subscription cleanup succeed, while sibling chats continue.
- Codex avoids repeated history preparation, defers unused catalogs and servers, and fixes the fleet configuration-copy path that exhausted memory. Failed commands and supported simple Code Mode actions retain saved outcomes; sandbox listings and copies preserve tabs and line breaks in filenames. Missed file notifications can still leave listings stale until restart. Older schema-1 sidecar bindings stay available for diagnosis but are no longer imported or converted.
- Codex startup diagnostics identify the stage reached, launcher and pending requests, while known process-wide log notices stop repeating in chats. A managed launcher failure needs installation repair and a Gateway restart to retry. After updating, run standalone Doctor to diagnose sandbox loopback restrictions in affected local Linux Docker or Podman runtimes; it does not make blocked shell commands succeed.

Claude CLI resumes pick up updated instructions, affected Ollama models regain tool execution with Tool Search, and Gemma 4 can recover complete standalone tool requests returned as text. ChatGPT also gains a bounded retry for a specific rejected WebSocket continuation, preserving completed tool results.

- After a long response reaches its output limit, the first corrective model request gets its existing recovery window from that request's start. Later retries share the same bounded deadline; ordinary provider and inactivity timeouts remain. [#167708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167708). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

Thanks [@robburke](https://github.com/robburke), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@yu-xin-c](https://github.com/yu-xin-c).
- OpenAI-compatible proxies with too little estimated room for an answer trigger context recovery or a clear error instead of returning an unusably short reply, including with thinking disabled. Conservative estimates may require a smaller prompt or a context setting that matches the server's allocation. See [local models](/gateway/local-models). [#167878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167878). Thanks [@amittell](https://github.com/amittell), [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization), [@glebsergeevich](https://github.com/glebsergeevich), [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed Gemma 4 replies can recover a complete standalone tool request returned as text at the end of an answer. Incomplete, cancelled, truncated or example text cannot authorize an action; direct mode and other model families are outside this recovery. [#167886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167886). Thanks [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Affected Ollama models can execute tools with Tool Search enabled instead of mistaking the tool-call wrapper for a control function. The correction covers known native and compatible chat envelopes; custom templates and other routes can still need attention. See [Ollama troubleshooting](/providers/ollama/troubleshooting). [#167933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167933). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Thanks [@shailaja-bot](https://github.com/shailaja-bot) for the related report and prompt that supported the parser investigation.
- A ChatGPT turn retries once with its full input after a specific cached WebSocket continuation is rejected before a response starts. Completed tool results carry forward without running the tools again. SSE, later failures and unrelated errors remain outside this repair. See [OpenAI continuation behavior](/providers/openai/advanced). [#168055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168055). Thanks [@nikosml](https://github.com/nikosml), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Slow native plugin checks can keep advancing until the model list is ready, and ordinary model-list preparation timeouts can recover. A stalled active native check still needs a manual reload or restart. Gateway diagnostics also inspect the installation you selected. [8b786700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8b786700801e24738e86740535ef57078bbeaac0). Thanks [@martinderm](https://github.com/martinderm), [@matthematics1137](https://github.com/matthematics1137), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@swampgorilla](https://github.com/swampgorilla).
- A failed model lookup with differently cased provider text no longer hides a later valid lookup; provider names remain case-sensitive. Vertex also uses Google Auth's rotated token for non-file application credentials. [99878430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/998784305885de579a83f71aef4bc3f60d4ba6e3). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Replies, scheduled tasks and Gateway agent requests keep their accepted model information while a background catalog changes. Cancellation, revoked access, closed leases and Gateway shutdown still stop work. See [model catalogs](/concepts/models). [df18524e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/df18524e3f9a41e4e00a6b583a811d9e0b1606c9). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex replies recover a missing ending when nonempty streamed text is an exact prefix of the completed answer. This does not recover missing middle text or a reply with no streamed text. [2bb51e77](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2bb51e77b0de34dda2a9c35bec830e4ed5bd67d1). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

</details>

### Automations and Scheduling

- Older scheduled jobs keep their original agent and delivery intent instead of silently changing during routine activity or edits. Run `openclaw doctor --fix` when prompted, or use the normal updater's Doctor phase. Affected jobs wait before running, editing or removal, with a due one-time occurrence preserved. Unknown delivery modes need review and an explicit choice in the editor before saving.
- Interrupted one-time messages and webhooks stay disabled with **Unknown** delivery status after restart when they may already have been sent, including jobs set to delete themselves. **Check the recipient before retrying**, because Unknown can mean sent or unsent. **Before upgrading, make a verified SQLite backup that includes its write-ahead log.** Older runtimes refuse migrated state outside the updater's temporary grace period; reverting the binary alone is insufficient. Restoring an older backup loses later delivery records and cannot undo messages or webhooks already sent.
- OpenClaw keeps handling events while task edits, results and scratch notes wait to save, preserving newer changes, recorded recipients and cancellation checks. Scheduled-session cleanup does less work in large stores, and background runs can reuse matching prepared plugins without changing their workspace or file-access boundaries. Removing a running job requests cancellation; the acknowledgment does not mean work has stopped or undo completed effects.
- Running only due jobs manually now follows the same retry backoff and trigger spacing as scheduling. Force can bypass timing and enablement, while active-run, ownership and delivery checks still apply. Stopping a scheduling source waits for callbacks underway. Logbook starts each repeat interval after the callback finishes, so longer callbacks lengthen its cadence; Team Reports keeps absolute due times.
- Gmail watchers can recover from short port conflicts during restart, and repeated rejected attempts to process the same email produce fewer duplicate notices while retries continue. For a persistent conflict, stop the occupying process and restart OpenClaw or the foreground watcher. Failure messages distinguish periodic checks from event-driven work and explain how to follow the logs.
- You can follow an isolated scheduled run from messages through agent activity and available billed usage in one trace with `diagnostics-otel`.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and changes</summary>

- Preserve historical job owners and delivery intent through Doctor repair, with verified backups and the pending occurrence intact. Within stored delivery objects, missing or null modes and the old `deliver` spelling become `announce`; unknown modes need review. Completely omitted delivery settings keep their normal defaults. Update rehearsals retain live legacy files for the real import. [d89da759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d89da7595d0d6c51f4e17b5631114023e49f931b) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Hold interrupted one-time message and webhook deliveries when replay could send them again; runs proven never to have started delivery remain recoverable. [#159873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159873) Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Reject malformed stored-job saves supplying only the obsolete identity alias before changing any bundled job rows. Supported jobs and public `jobId` RPC parameters keep their behavior. [#163247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163247) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recheck current job state after another process causes an edit to be refused, allowing a later edit once that run finishes while retaining ownership checks. [0193905d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0193905dfa3c8c49f12d4fa432c4e20ef38d3848) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep Gateway events moving while completed manual or timer jobs wait to save their results, with job and execution records updated together. [#161142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161142) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save task edits in background workers while preserving newer edits and run markers. Confirmed changes are not replayed if a worker reply is lost, although the caller can still see a reply-loss error. [#161241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161241) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save scratch notes and heartbeat proposals without blocking the Gateway thread; queued saves refuse changed jobs, revoked permissions and stale originating turns. [#161332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161332) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce periodic scheduled-session cleanup pauses in large stores by avoiding unnecessary loading of ordinary sessions' saved snapshots. [#161510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161510) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@todddickerson](https://github.com/todddickerson).
- Further reduce scheduled-session cleanup work by selecting relevant saved data before decoding it, retaining the same checks and retention rules. [#161645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161645) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save results for jobs with long histories while holding the shared database writer lock for less time, and identify the operation behind slow writes more clearly. [#161655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161655) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Use less temporary memory when repeatedly listing automations or reading restart status by reusing unchanged snapshots. [#164160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164160) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Disclose active-run cancellation requests when removing an automation, retaining run history. An admitted run can remove its own job without cancelling itself. [#159586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159586) Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [@dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keep skipped-run history and startup notices tied to the agent and recipient captured when saved, including an absent default recipient. Interrupted cleanup avoids stuck local reservations and replay of uncertain writes. [#161703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161703) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Check execution records away from the Gateway thread and stop the next affected event, result publication or webhook if its job is removed during that check. [#161935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161935) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid redundant plugin loading for Gateway background runs in other workspaces and for matching disabled or failed plugin records. Execution directories and file-access restrictions remain in place. [#164594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164594) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve stream-job status and nondecreasing loss counters when sources change or shutdown loses a worker reply. Obsolete updates cannot overwrite a replacement stream, and uncertain outcomes remain errors. [#161502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161502) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Align manual due-only timing with scheduled retries and settle started work when a scheduling source stops. Sibling schedules continue, and elapsed stream deadlines are handled once after sleep. [#163228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163228) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tie Logbook and Team Reports schedules to service lifetimes, waiting for admitted work before closing reporting resources. Logbook uses intervals after completion, while Team Reports retains absolute due times and its existing cancellation deadline. [#163918](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163918) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retry Gmail watcher port conflicts after 5, 10 and 20 seconds for managed watchers and the foreground command, recovering from short restart overlaps. [#161503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161503) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@kazuyuki-eguchi](https://github.com/kazuyuki-eguchi).
- Suppress repeat failure notices when the same retained email cannot start processing within five minutes, while retries and diagnostics continue. Expiry, eviction or restart can permit another notice. Failures after processing starts remain visible. [#164206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164206) Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@TrevorDGreen33](https://github.com/TrevorDGreen33).
- Describe a failed heartbeat as an incomplete background check, with `openclaw logs --follow` troubleshooting guidance and optional diagnostic details governed by disclosure settings. [#162869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162869) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Match failure wording to the selected work, so event-driven requests do not falsely appear to be failed periodic checks. Selected periodic tasks and monitor checks retain heartbeat wording. [ac85f6dc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ac85f6dc13ab6572f3f564a9fde920e4dd6a4d6e) Thanks [@juyterman1000](https://github.com/juyterman1000) and [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Point the Automations index directly to the existing [mapped-webhook instructions](/automation/cron-jobs/webhooks#mapped-hooks-post-hooks-name) for custom `POST /hooks/<name>` routes. [0ad243f9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0ad243f936f18008dfd307cc90fa4f7e18a44a1e) Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
- Keep an isolated scheduled run's activity and available billed usage together in its diagnostic trace when using `diagnostics-otel`. [#161304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161304) Thanks [@NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst), [@ryan-dyer-sp](https://github.com/ryan-dyer-sp) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>

### Browser and Computer Use

- Managed browsers can find installed Playwright Chromium on Linux ARM64 and when `XDG_CACHE_HOME` redirects the cache. Browser Doctor checks the launch settings your profile actually uses, including remote, attached and customized browsers.
- Native Codex application control no longer reports a false browser-authentication error for API-key installations. Setup keeps its timeout allowance across clock changes, and agents get instructions for locating windows and providing browser and page references together.
- Mac desktop automation uses Peekaboo 4.7.0. If an exact-window menu action cannot be dispatched safely, `actionOnly` refuses it; use the supported `actionFirst` fallback.
- Browser connection addresses keep their explicit default port even when a path, query or fragment contains an at-sign. Firecrawl fetches also honor `--format text` through both `infer web fetch` and `capability web fetch`, though some Markdown formatting can remain.
- Tab cleanup and Dashboard reconciliation continue after the request that started them ends, until the Browser service stops. Cleanup retries produce fewer repeated warnings for stopped profiles, and rate-limit errors return without hanging on response cancellation. See [browser configuration](/tools/browser/configuration).
- Desktop reconnects no longer accumulate repeated attempts to clean up a failed session. **Reconnecting alone no longer retries a cleanup failure**, even if it was transient. Inspect the reported process or filesystem error and perform lifecycle cleanup, such as turning Host Desktop off in Labs; a replacement starts only after cleanup succeeds. See [desktop configuration](/gateway/config-browser-ui-desktop).
- Keeping private-network browser access requires the canonical `browser.ssrfPolicy.dangerouslyAllowPrivateNetwork` setting. Replace the retired `browser.ssrfPolicy.allowPrivateNetwork` key if your configuration still uses it; the old browser key is no longer honored.
- Browser inspection shows the registered tool, and sandbox reservation and cleanup work moves into the background while keeping its order. Old unconfigured `browser/clawd` directories now stay on disk instead of being offered for Doctor archival; configured profiles remain supported.

### Plugins and Integrations

- **Plugin upgrades.** Keep affected plugins working by updating them before the OpenClaw host; the [SDK migration guide](/plugins/sdk-migration) explains retired imports and replacements. Legacy extension-only installs need an explicit supported installation or 2026.9.7 Doctor repair before upgrading. Updated Zoom, Teams, ClickClack, Google Chat, IRC, Slack, SMS and Zalo packages using the refactored helpers require host 2026.9.8 or newer. Historical conversation bindings without recognized plugin ownership can refuse reassignment, with recovery still unresolved.
- **Loading and reloads.** You can read model lists, sign-in status and session metadata while a plugin reload waits for work to finish. If that wait fails, retry `openclaw plugins reload <id> --wait` or revert pending settings; a model call blocking its own reload fails promptly and can be retried afterward. Loading avoids repeated native-plugin scans and supports managed npm layouts across filesystems and symlinks. After editing bundled TypeScript, restart OpenClaw; compiled installations also need a rebuild first.
- **Background work and older files.** Stopping or replacing a service cancels pending callbacks and waits for work already admitted after owner cleanup. Before this upgrade, migrate old Device Pair notify files, Discord model/thread files and iMessage reply, sent-echo or catchup files through 2026.9.5 and Doctor; Voice Call `calls.jsonl` needs 2026.9.7. Verify migration before backing up or moving the preserved originals and retrying. Standalone Voice Call keeps its webhook serving after printing a result; SIGINT or SIGTERM ends it and finishes cleanup.
- **Cloud workers and GitHub.** Worker cleanup and re-enrollment respect your selected profile and platform, with bounded retries for qualifying timeouts. Worker and remote-proof paths require Crabbox 0.69.0 or newer. Definitively unsupported Linux snapshot captures can use existing recovery; older paused captures still need recovery. GitHub publication choices and status stay readable during conflicting snapshot activity. Follow [cloud-worker troubleshooting](/gateway/cloud-workers/troubleshooting) and retain `--lease-id` when a command refuses to proceed.
- **Tool discovery and input.** [Tool Search](/tools/tool-search) finds tools with trusted arguments nested in combined schemas. Repeated tool or skill searches reuse unchanged indexes. Inputs keep referenced types and defaults and accept custom strings alongside presets; overly nested MCP results explain the need for flatter output. Meeting captions and Logbook skip completed history, though their first index build can take time and temporary disk space.
- **Integration setup and recovery.** Voice Call keeps an active call's recorded agent; hang up old unowned calls through the provider and start a new call. `openclaw doctor --fix` repairs legacy MCP transport aliases, and Gmail setup accepts Python paths with spaces. Supported Ollama models sent directly to `ollama.com` can use maximum thinking; local and local `:cloud` relay routes keep the compatible high mapping. The removed Diffs Language Pack `api.ts` entrypoint may affect external imports; replacement guidance remains unresolved.

External plugins avoid unnecessary reloads during model-catalog adoption. Installed-plugin startup repeats fewer file reads, Windows uses a bounded copy path for eligible small files, and valid plugins can load through ancestor directory junctions. Changed or incomplete captures remain rejected.

<summary>Changes and sources</summary>

- The `channel-lifecycle`, `channel-message`, `channel-reply-pipeline`, `config-runtime` and `infra-runtime` imports are retired, with replacement calls documented in the migration guide. [#162333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162333). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The `command-auth`, `discord` and `telegram-account` SDK facades are retired; use the supported channel contracts and injected runtime. [#163366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163366). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugins using the retired Copilot token-exchange helpers must move to registered authentication hooks and provider-owned endpoint resolution. [#163587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163587). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Provider plugins replace eight retired stream/replay constants with the existing family constructors; the [compatibility guide](/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy) lists replacements and retained aliases. [#163591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163591). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The refactored Zoom and Teams plugin packages require host 2026.9.8 or newer. [#161628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161628). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updated ClickClack, Google Chat, IRC, Slack, SMS and Zalo packages using the shared secret helper require host 2026.9.8 or newer. [#162827](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162827). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Old extension-only npm stubs need an explicit supported installation or pre-upgrade Doctor repair. [#163231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163231). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Dylanzhang1128](https://github.com/Dylanzhang1128), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Bundled source plugins share loaded host code, so source edits take effect after restarting the Gateway. [#163639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163639). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Supported older Codex harness plugins retain `sourceVisibleReplies`; `visibleReplies` is the canonical field and explicit message configuration takes precedence. [#163497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163497), [#163786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163786). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Typed approval hooks must omit the retired `requireApproval.timeoutBehavior` field; `timeoutMs` and `timeoutReason` remain supported. [#163333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163333). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Typed configuration uses `models[ref].agentRuntime` and `agents.defaults.compaction` instead of the retired per-agent fields. [#164586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164586). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The newer meeting page-script factory uses `statusPrelude` and `statusCall` descriptors; older standalone builders remain available. [#163983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163983). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Installed QA runner plugins using legacy-only registration must move their runner registration to `qa-runner-api.ts` or `.js`. [#164058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164058). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Feishu broadcasts and agent selection accept canonical keyed rosters, while source types reject retired roster/default fields earlier. [#164103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164103). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixed-proxy helper users should review zero-valued connection options. Most use Node defaults, but `maxTotalSockets: 0` fails during construction. [#161624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161624). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Callable provider catalogs retain lifetime checks, and historical ownerless conversation bindings now refuse reassignment rather than entering the old migration flow. [#164047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164047). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Migration-index links reach the matching replacement-API sections directly. [80f68b8862fc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/80f68b8862fc98c843df31040af3219b6b8b99ed). Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
- Plugin reloads keep passive model, sign-in and chat metadata available through waiting and failed drains. [#162301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162301). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session lists, inspection and health can use the active thinking policy while a retired registry transitions to its replacement. [#162470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162470). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

- Reply model lookup honors disabled plugins for custom providers, aliases and model overrides. [120b43633fde](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/120b43633fde174329b35cb6017fad31cac635d3). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Bun plugins resolve valid wildcard package-import aliases with filename suffixes. [#163232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163232). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Quarantined-tool and context-engine health reads move off the calling thread while continuing to report stored status. [#161375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161375). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and scheduled cleanup warnings preserve both filesystem and ownership-token release failures. [#161488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161488). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Stream and result handoff removes repeated boundary wrapping; plugin authors retain responsibility for leaving delivered payloads unchanged. [#163818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163818). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Native hooks retry brief database locks within the existing deadline before declaring their authenticated bridge unavailable. [#161822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161822). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Context-engine startup and reload wait for accepted health cleanup without letting an obsolete activation clear a newer failure. [#161952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161952). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Standalone SDK sandbox preparation refuses shared state owned by a live or unverified runtime. Use the owning runtime, or stop it through the service owner and wait for embedded activity before retrying offline. [#164203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164203). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Remote-exec bootstrap includes required published `.setup` runtime chunks for affected external plugins such as Codex. [#165669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165669). Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@JakeBotFleet](https://github.com/JakeBotFleet).
- CLI help and command discovery release executable-owned plugin metadata resources and retain reported cleanup failures. [bfdb432570ac](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bfdb432570aca3797c8c42230ae2488959381214). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Completed disposal can finish shutdown while still reporting its error; unfinished cleanup and failed prerequisites remain blocking. [cf78e6443477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cf78e64434775321f863c8c55369979d3ae90d07). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved Control UI plugin descriptors remain readable after retirement without invoking the retired plugin. [dc8393473d09](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/dc8393473d094e033eab94a66f5c51db0c6b4dc6). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Catalog-created resource controls keep their factory's plugin context, including the repaired speech-stream release callback. [a388b8f1a736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a388b8f1a736201531399571af7b8209569cd392). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Worker-runtime imports resolve through the registered main, worker and lifecycle package entry points. [6c31d857a79c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6c31d857a79c9cca42b7d8bf686d36a91851eb03). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Installed-plugin ownership and conflict checks reuse resolved paths within each check. [6fc676c5b563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6fc676c5b563072bd29700ca0e6bb2937c195086). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Service/account scheduling cancels pending work and joins admitted callbacks after owner cleanup, with the separate legacy-file upgrade requirements above. [7f0410c2e66e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7f0410c2e66e912a524e72ca883deef2dca09d0f). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Claw cleanup and MCP setup-status saving run in the background; cleanup waits until its ownership record is saved. [#163112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163112). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stopping Beam and Codex desktop watchers cancels polls and retries and waits for admitted work, preventing late generation publication. [#163914](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163914). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Custom legacy PCM Talk integrations avoid starting an obsolete reply when cancellation arrives after its replacement. [#163732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163732). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Global MCP cleanup continues reporting an earlier unconfirmed disposal instead of losing that uncertainty on a later empty cleanup. [a6089edc5cd9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a6089edc5cd9f53f98313b4392d3fc1566258331). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Crabbox 0.69.0 or newer supports recovery from definitive unsupported Linux snapshot capture without treating every capture error as a fallback. [#161465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161465), [#162334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162334). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A healthy macOS Crabbox worker can re-enroll on a busy host without a false reprovisioning error. [#162233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162233). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Crabbox cleanup stays with the selected profile, and concurrent requests share executable preparation. [#164128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164128). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Linux profiles request Linux explicitly, and sanitized worker errors retain useful status and recent log details. [#164129](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164129). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Qualifying coordinator timeouts get at most three attempts within the original budget; fresh unsupported-backend refusals identify the profile problem. [#164231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164231). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- GitHub publication account choices and status remain readable during conflicting retained-snapshot activity. [#161551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161551). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Personal GitHub confirmation status follows session archive/restore and rejects reads made stale by archive changes. [#141994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141994). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tool Search discovers trusted tool arguments inside combined schemas and tuple items. See [Tool Search](/tools/tool-search). [#164032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164032). Thanks [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Repeated tool and installed-skill searches reuse an unchanged word index across turns and Code Mode cells. [#164166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164166). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Control UI guidance explains the rendered tab, widget and link-reader contribution surfaces. See [host hooks](/plugins/sdk-overview/host-hooks). [#147599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147599). Thanks [@Colton-Harris](https://github.com/Colton-Harris), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Client guidance distinguishes session metadata, recent stored messages and display-normalized history. See [session RPC readers](/gateway/protocol/rpc-session-control). [#155449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155449). Thanks [@winddd666](https://github.com/winddd666), [@datus1982](https://github.com/datus1982).
- Affected plugin and MCP tool schemas accept valid custom strings alongside named presets. [#159755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159755). Thanks [@Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma).
- Overly nested MCP results fail with flatter-output guidance, including consistent [Code Mode output](/tools/code-mode/output) errors. [#160825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160825). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@hpyhandsome](https://github.com/hpyhandsome).
- Encoded [plugin schema references](/plugins/manifest) retain separators and defaults; use `~1` for a literal slash in a key and `~0` for a tilde. [#161954](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161954). Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Encoded local tool references retain input types and supported numeric conversions while rejecting invalid text. [#162435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162435). Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
- Native plugin preparation reuses successful checks on unchanged captured files in affected hardlink installations. [#162254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162254). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@WG-Mojo](https://github.com/WG-Mojo), [@eddiekk](https://github.com/eddiekk), [@NickM83](https://github.com/NickM83).
- Bun-backed npm plugin operations use bundled npm 12.1.0 while preserving explicit Git and remote-tarball source restrictions. [#162581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162581). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Running Bun plugins retain selected dependency contents reached through supported package aliases after source edits. [#164223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164223). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeated configuration checks avoid compiling temporary conditional-default validators again. [#164208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164208). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Meeting caption retries use a narrower index while exact retries stay deduplicated and revisions remain distinct. [#164127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164127). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Logbook pending-analysis polls avoid scanning completed history while preserving pending order and retries. [#164130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164130). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Lobster plugin's runtime 2026.9.14 update corrects structured LLM task request/result translation. [#162558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162558). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Comfy guidance separates hosted MCP account sign-in from workflow-plugin API keys. See [MCP OAuth](/cli/mcp/transports#oauth-workflow) and [Comfy MCP](https://docs.comfy.org/agent-tools/mcp). [#163197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163197). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- QA Lab Multipass accepts a valid repository child such as `..qa-artifacts` while rejecting parent, root and symlink escapes. [#163223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163223). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- QuickJS Code Mode cards and setup use the shared white-background mascot. [#163804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163804). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Voice Call preserves recorded ownership and leaves old unowned active calls for provider-side hangup and a fresh call. [#163118](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163118). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor repairs old MCP `type` aliases, and an explicit canonical transport wins over a conflicting legacy type. [#162256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162256). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- MCP listings preserve opaque pagination tokens, and supported models sent directly to `ollama.com` receive maximum thinking without changing local-relay compatibility. [db0c17463643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/db0c17463643179148600aa03be5f1f0cc5815b2).
- Gmail setup keeps the full discovered Python executable path when its directories contain spaces. [fd333c7e1773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fd333c7e17731cfbb28375b58110b4cd30d3f52a). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The [Firecrawl guide](/tools/firecrawl) links directly to the provider website and API-key dashboard. [#157257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157257). Thanks [@JuampiHernandez](https://github.com/JuampiHernandez).
- Malformed Claude Workshop replies reject the waiting MCP request promptly instead of leaving it until the SDK timeout. [#158916](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158916). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Diffs screenshots honor the readiness deadline, Canvas assets can retry after a transient read failure, and failed Twitch startup clears its deadline timer. Diffs Language Pack authors should check imports of the removed top-level `api.ts`; a replacement is unverified. [#161096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161096). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved ClickClack discussion updates begin with service startup rather than falling back to premature polling. [#161150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161150). Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Canvas reconnects avoid duplicate actions, Logbook rejects reversed trimmed intervals, Tavily skips impossible dates, and failed LanceDB table discovery closes its connection. [#161706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161706). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory Wiki imports backtick-heavy text, and Plivo V3 verifies valid webhooks with prototype-named parameters. [#162425](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162425). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Parallel search caches distinguish delimiter-sensitive requests and an explicitly named `default` session. [#163911](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163911). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Adopting a downloaded model catalog reuses unchanged external plugins instead of reloading them and making the Gateway unresponsive shortly after startup. Initial plugin loading and explicit reloads still do their own work. [#167544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167544). Thanks [@cmrstudio56](https://github.com/cmrstudio56), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Installed-plugin startup repeats fewer ordinary-file reads. On Windows, eligible small ordinary plugin files also use the existing bounded copy path; large and native files retain their guarded path. Changed or incomplete plugin captures remain rejected. [#168017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168017), [#168256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168256). Thanks [@gollymashed](https://github.com/gollymashed), [@krumch](https://github.com/krumch), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@RuiCatalao7](https://github.com/RuiCatalao7), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@utesara](https://github.com/utesara).
- Windows can load valid installed plugins when an ancestor Node directory redirects through a junction. Entries outside the verified package root remain rejected. [052baa8c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/052baa8ce55fdd45316e58d3f6b8889a19421520). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor uses an updated plugin's own repair callbacks and settings rules. Preparing large plugins can use fewer disk writes on supported filesystems while later edits stay independent. Reload verifies copied content and asks you to retry if files change during preparation. [7039983b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7039983bd7909452ff4cb2a4b3ad2251678f4e22), [dd51d9cc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/dd51d9ccd5a83ea810e85907403b7ba2a0f29d25). Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- MCP library updates bind saved sign-in records to their authorization server. If that server changes, reconnect explicitly, including when older client information was saved. [f9310588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f9310588a85ab3acaf17248e360f050b26c3c6af). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Plugin state operations continue through harmless concurrent conversation edits, while still refusing work after the active session writer changes. [01c06759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/01c06759eb299ac56c07dafe742f542915cdc884). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Valid Gateway startup avoids unnecessary diagnostic-module loading for affected external plugins. Invalid configuration and explicit Doctor still receive the plugin's repair checks. See [running the Gateway](/cli/gateway/running). [a84bd1a5](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a84bd1a593ff44246ce8222b1f488bbc54ee98fd). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

</details>

### Security and Privacy

- Guests can again launch private sandboxed helpers, notify their child agents and ask agents to rename conversations they created without broad write access. Shared work follows the chosen verified participant's permissions and the original run's limits, within a shared trust boundary. On multi-user installations, only creators or administrators can archive or restore threads; assigned noncreators lose those actions after upgrading, including on existing threads.
- Sharing controls, agent discovery and command lists recheck access after delays or reconnects. Session read access works for shared GitHub publication options and receipts, while automation hides private working notes after access or ownership changes.
