- Approval links show supplied payment, recipient, posting and automation details before you decide. Unrelated edits preserve pending approvals; relevant authority changes invalidate them. Commands suppressing audit output follow ordinary read/write approval policy, including eligible automatic review.
- Pairing and execution-approval settings use the running Gateway's current state. A newer CLI refuses an older Gateway without the required support; update it or stop it through its service owner before retrying. Inspect current requests or settings before repeating a mutation whose result is uncertain. For a narrowed browser token, recover administrator access with `openclaw dashboard --json` on the Gateway host and open the fresh URL in the same browser, origin and Gateway within ten minutes.
- Work on a node respects current [workspace and patch-tool restrictions](/nodes/session-hosting). Eligible native apps on the same machine avoid repeated initial approvals under existing local policy, and reused writable SSH sandboxes receive new uploads. Strict filesystem mode can refuse recursive cleanup on Windows or Linux without `openat2`, leaving directories behind; use a platform supporting the required protection. Migrate old filesystem mode variable names to their native-mode equivalents.
- Credential prompts confirm a completed save, even when an accepted save finishes privately after reset. If runtime refresh then fails, use `secrets.reload` instead of submitting the answer again. Older core secret references without a provider require `openclaw doctor --fix`. Preserve the original backups and inspect the repair, especially for Talk and realtime Talk, whose setup can discard an older providerless API-key object before repair.
- Logout removes copied catalog credentials from still-saved profiles, and secret-excluded Git backups omit catalog keys and headers. Profiles already removed by older logouts and previously created or pushed backups need separate inspection. Permitted CLI commands and literal-loopback HTTP requests work with the secret egress proxy; secret substitution remains refused over HTTP. Provider errors hide reflected credentials, shared OAuth profiles avoid unnecessary copies, and A2A status identifies token settings needing repair.
- Secret masking preserves ordinary text after `pass:` and reduces the affected long-output pauses, including with custom patterns. Chunked custom scanning can miss cross-boundary matches and treat chunk starts as anchors; not every pattern avoids stalls. Fallback diagnostics also mask secrets. Reserved private runtime entries stay out of compaction summaries and saved CLI notes without rewriting transcripts; an unresolved resumed-session issue may discard newly queued text or images in the affected legacy-context case.
- Starting OpenClaw with its managed Bun runtime or the macOS bundled runtime no longer installs missing packages implicitly. Install dependencies explicitly; existing services receive the launch flag through normal reinstall or update, and direct stock-Bun launches should use `--no-install`. Private-network Matrix and Mattermost settings need Doctor repair on 2026.9.7 before upgrading; Tlon retains update-time migration, while direct replacement needs `openclaw doctor --fix`. See [Bun installation](/install/bun) and the [configuration migration guide](/gateway/doctor/config-migrations).

Protected-credential guidance explains whether the planned action can use a secret before asking you to store it. It adds no SSH or sudo password consumer; deliberately readable Gateway environment entries retain their separate behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Shared turns and their children use the chosen verified participant's permissions, bounded by the original run. Model-account credentials stay the same, and permissions can be revoked during retained work. [#160525](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160525). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Guests with own-session write permission can launch hidden sandboxed helpers and receive their results, including after yielding. The helper retains the initiating person's model and sandbox restrictions. [#163367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163367). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Session-only writers can notify an authorized child they own without starting a new run. Notifications live in memory for the process lifetime, so restarting does not provide durable delivery. [#162986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162986). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Guests can ask agents to change the displayed name of a conversation they created, including under default sandbox policy. Explicit tool restrictions still apply, and renaming another created conversation requires its durable session ID. [#163421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163421). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- In multi-user installations, only a thread's creator or an administrator can archive or restore it. This also applies to existing threads; being assigned responsibility alone no longer grants that permission. See [multi-user conversations](/concepts/multi-user). [#160932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160932). Thanks [@stevenlee-oai](https://github.com/stevenlee-oai).
- Session-scoped users can read shared GitHub publication options and receipts for conversations they may view. Options require a supported current managed repository or worktree; personal accounts and publication mutations retain their own permissions. [#162984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162984). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Shared GitHub publication options remain readable when both the connection grant and role imply session read using different permission names. Later role revocation still prevents the read. [#163910](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163910). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agent discovery follows the current role and waits for a fresh roster after reconnecting. Draft identity edits survive when they still refer to the same profile and intent, and a Stop/completion race no longer leaves a capacity slot occupied. [#161019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161019). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Sharing controls check fresh stored permissions and visibility after waiting. Outdated membership or ownership cannot carry a request into a replacement conversation, and stale cached visibility no longer turns a needed update into a no-op. [#163572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163572). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Command and skill lists for shared conversations are withheld if access, the conversation or selected skill revisions change during discovery. [#163643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163643). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delayed owner, membership and suggestion changes reject outdated permissions. Cancelled or revoked suggestions cannot become new input; already accepted suggestions can settle without becoming available for replay. Provider-review pauses block new suggestions and edits while allowing dismissal and accepted settlement. [ef27c1fc2b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ef27c1fc2be3a68c8904e12cf6fe909f05fe4f5d). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Authorized agent work no longer fails a false permission check when runtime modules load independently or reload. Revocation, cancellation and scope limits still apply. [#163725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163725). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Private automation working notes are withheld after caller access is revoked or ownership changes. Older jobs also avoid false conflicts caused by their creation metadata. [#161387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161387). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Standalone approval pages display supplied payment amounts and recipients, automation terms and duration, message recipients or posting destinations. This supplies decision context without granting permission. [#164534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164534). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Approvals bound to a request survive unrelated agent and secret-store edits. Relevant permission or routing changes permanently revoke the pending approval, even if those settings are later restored. [#160856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160856). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Commands that suppress audit output use the ordinary approval policy for both reads and writes. Eligible requests can use automatic review instead of a special human-only prompt; explicit denial, allowlists and always-ask policy remain effective. [#161718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161718). Thanks [@jesse-merhi](https://github.com/jesse-merhi).
- Listing and revoking standing scheduled-command grants moves database administration off the Gateway main thread, with permission checks around changes. Grant validation and consumption at command launch retain their existing path. See [execution approvals](/tools/exec-approvals). [#162549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162549). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Pairing list/approve and default approvals get/set/allowlist edits use authenticated Gateway routing or exclusive offline ownership. Refusal, missing credentials or a lost reply never triggers an automatic local replay; explicit Gateway and node targeting remains available. [#164075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164075). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepared node commands with invalid file bindings or policy metadata stay rejected before execution approval. Retired top-level command-text fallbacks are removed; supported `plan.rawCommand` remains the canonical input. [#164544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164544). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Authorized GitHub publication can include unchanged Actions workflows inherited exactly from the verified target when ancestry is unambiguous. Authored workflow edits and conflict resolutions still require full write authority. [#162396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162396). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A browser using a narrowed token explains insufficient permissions instead of offering a futile Retry. To recover administrator access, run `openclaw dashboard --json` on the Gateway host and open its fresh browser URL in the same browser, origin and Gateway within ten minutes. [#162951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162951). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A CLI `/btw` side-question execution context closes even when backend cleanup fails, so later work cannot reuse it. The cleanup error remains visible. [#162070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162070). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unexpected `openclaw security audit --fix` errors stop before the audit and return exit status 1. JSON callers receive the [CLI failure response](/cli#json-failures); run a separate `openclaw security audit` to inspect the current state. [#164159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164159). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Security audits retire the model-age and provider-tier advisories `models.legacy` and `models.weak_tier`. Structured-report consumers should account for those removed IDs; the separate small-model tool-exposure check remains. [ecb897146e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ecb897146e5c47a6cde4e0d5552ce7d70c3700c3). Thanks [@jesse-merhi](https://github.com/jesse-merhi).
- Node turns receive the current filesystem, model and patch-tool policy from the Gateway. Global and agent-specific workspace-only and `applyPatch` settings apply with session-permission precedence, and warm workers refresh their tool catalog for each turn. See the [cloud-worker security model](/gateway/cloud-workers/security-model). [10ea4739bd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/10ea4739bd0f3d72663fd924197530bef1c4f6b6). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex host-backed file bridges that adopt the supported access contract check actual file targets for reads, opens and metadata. Denied aliases and targets changed after preparation are refused; this contract does not cover every bridge or file operation. [#150731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150731). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- New uploads reach reused writable SSH sandboxes, with managed originals and preprocessing or preview files retained for restart. Read-only sandboxes continue to reject writes; this covers inbound uploads. [#158026](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158026). Thanks [@Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@DaveFan-NCHC](https://github.com/DaveFan-NCHC), [@FelixIsaac](https://github.com/FelixIsaac).
- Strict `OPENCLAW_FS_SAFE_NATIVE_MODE=require` uses stronger native checks for file mutations. Recursive cleanup can refuse on Windows or Linux without `openat2`, leaving directories behind; use a supported platform when those guarantees are required. Default automatic mode remains best effort. [#160617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160617). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Filesystem upgrades preserve legacy `require` settings instead of silently relaxing them and add the Windows dangling-symlink exclusive-create safeguard. Move `FS_SAFE_PYTHON_MODE` and `OPENCLAW_FS_SAFE_PYTHON_MODE` to their `*_NATIVE_MODE` names. Remove `FS_SAFE_PYTHON`, `OPENCLAW_FS_SAFE_PYTHON`, `OPENCLAW_PINNED_PYTHON` and `OPENCLAW_PINNED_WRITE_PYTHON`. Deprecated Plugin SDK hints remain accepted but ignored; direct fs-safe consumers must use `configureFsSafeNative` and `FsSafeNativeConfig` without `pythonPath`. [#163408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163408). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows plugin paths reject foreign shares and ambiguous device paths before inspection. Use an admitted relative path or the root's host/share spelling for a rejected alias; removing namespace prefixes is not a safe repair. [#162574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162574). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An already-paired local native app can add its first node role with the same key under the existing local auto-approval policy, including an eligible pending retry. Token replacement, revocation, remote and browser connections retain approval requirements. See [pairing](/gateway/pairing). [#163730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163730). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible silently paired same-machine native nodes can receive their initial permitted capabilities without an extra review panel, including an app in remote mode whose actual connection is local. `autoApproveLocal=false` retains manual approval; later capability additions, command denies, OS permissions and execution approvals still apply. [#163862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163862). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Secret-setting operations run through database workers and wait for acknowledged persistence. Credential questions publish only the safe stored marker; accepted saves survive reset, and compensating rollback cannot overwrite a newer writer's entry. [31806d84f3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/31806d84f3bb35b4389fe06ed3963734d01855cf). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Core credential readers require explicit `source`, `provider` and `id`. Doctor repairs registered legacy paths and reports removed extra fields; public SDK input adapters still accept plain providerless references, while extended providerless auth inputs reject before saving. Keep extra metadata outside reference objects. Run `openclaw doctor --fix` for older core references and preserve original backups; Talk and realtime Talk may discard a providerless API-key object before repair. [2c0278b46c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2c0278b46cd29614fc08f65a1632f4f863146f4e). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Profile logout scrubs matching copied catalog credentials while the profile is still saved, and prevents in-flight work from restoring them. Profiles already removed by an older logout cannot be retroactively cleaned this way; an incomplete guarded restoration needs profile inspection. [#143647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143647). Thanks [@jason-allen-oneal](https://github.com/jason-allen-oneal), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi), [@CHAESH](https://github.com/CHAESH), [@matthewmoroz](https://github.com/matthewmoroz), [@sunhsiao](https://github.com/sunhsiao), [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).
- Agent command environments and configured secret values are read in a worker instead of querying on the Gateway main thread. Commands reuse one snapshot per run and refresh next run; cancellation prevents launch without poisoning later reuse. Snapshots over 32 MiB fail visibly instead of supplying a partial environment, and individual values retain the 64 KiB limit. [#164242](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164242). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Permitted CLI commands work with the secret egress proxy by using the admitted run context. Existing approvals and retired-run restrictions continue to apply. [#160760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160760). Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- The secret egress proxy can forward permitted HTTP requests to literal `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` and `[::1]` addresses. DNS aliases are excluded, and secret substitution remains refused over HTTP. See [secret storage and egress](/gateway/secrets/secret-store-and-egress). [#160974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160974). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- LM Studio loading and Copilot discovery or embedding errors mask credentials reflected from encoded request headers. Chutes also accepts the IPv6 loopback OAuth callback `[::1]` while retaining the redirect URI. [#162488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162488). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Multi-agent OAuth synchronization visits the shared credential owner before sibling agents, preserving inheritance without unnecessary new credential copies. Previously duplicated stored credentials are not removed by this fix. [#163243](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163243). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The secrets index opens the existing [Bitwarden Secrets Manager](/gateway/secrets/integration-examples#bitwarden-secrets-manager-bws) and [password-store](/gateway/secrets/integration-examples#password-store-pass) recipes at their correct sections. [721efcefc0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/721efcefc0c79af2ec7e2af21c47f283e8ee6601). Thanks [@Shenrui-Ma](https://github.com/Shenrui-Ma).
- A2A status identifies peers with unresolved token settings and tells operators what to repair before reload or restart. Automatic plugin setup retains credential-resolution facts through configuration rewrites, and adding another peer preserves unavailable peers in saved configuration. Deliberately tokenless outbound peers retain anonymous sending. [cb24ec1fa7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cb24ec1fa7d5ce1d7162eedec3dc9447d391e6a8). Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- Ordinary prose following `pass:` remains readable, while supported password records and affected long-input Bun cases keep their default masking. Previously lost words in stored output are not restored. [#160888](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160888). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Built-in token masking handles affected long strings containing plus signs, slashes or equals signs without the previous stalls. [#161089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161089). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Custom `logging.redactPatterns` use 16 KiB chunks again. Matches spanning chunks can be missed, and anchored patterns apply at each chunk's start; built-in data-URL handling remains separate. [#161514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161514). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- WebSocket log values use secret masking and length limits when JSON serialization fails. Invalid log-level warnings also mask values recognized by the existing redactors before writing to stderr. [#164177](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164177). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Secret masking reuses compiled matchers to reduce temporary regular-expression allocations while keeping the same masked output. [#164152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164152). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cleanup of old generated-HTML trust records preserves a newer record written for the same path while filesystem inspection waits. Existing exact-byte trust and path restrictions continue to apply. [#164424](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164424). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reserved private runtime-context entries are excluded when compaction summaries and saved CLI notes read existing transcripts, including entries lacking replay metadata or opting out of replay. Stored transcript bytes are not rewritten. In the affected legacy-context case, resumed sessions may discard newly queued text or images; resolution of that issue is unconfirmed. [#164561](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164561). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed Bun services and OpenClaw-owned subprocesses fail locally on missing imports instead of installing packages at runtime. User commands, `bunx`, arbitrary third-party launchers and worker-thread resolution remain outside this launch policy. [#163270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163270). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The bundled macOS runtime disables implicit package installation across helpers and children using that executable. Explicit installation controls remain available. Its dependency update also reduces duplicate file-watch events and fixes queued WebSocket upgrades, event-listener exception propagation, process-title visibility and worker heap reporting. [#163313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163313). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix, Mattermost and Tlon use the supported nested private-network setting; an old flat key no longer grants access or defeats it. Matrix still permits access when either the root or account-level canonical opt-in is true, so account-level false does not override root-level true. [#163535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163535). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Project `.env` files no longer control the eight Homebrew download, source and configuration variables involved in installation. Move needed overrides to the launching shell or global environment; ordinary project preferences remain supported. [#150912](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150912). Thanks [@mmaps](https://github.com/mmaps).
- Docker builds from a source checkout exclude root and nested `.crabbox` directories, keeping their transient credentials and per-run state out of build images. [#164163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164163). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The locked dependency graph uses patched `source-map-js` 1.2.2 for the indexed-source-map denial-of-service advisory GHSA-68fv-2mgg-jv7q. [6d86072f8c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6d86072f8c4cede7419d9b53f84dd85430c33ce4). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), with review by [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

- Agents explain whether a protected credential has a supported consumer before asking you to store it. Protected credentials support authorized outbound HTTPS requests, not SSH or sudo passwords, subprocess input or shell substitution. Existing `--kind env` entries are deliberately readable and apply to Gateway execution. See [secrets guidance](/tools/secrets) and [CLI options](/cli/secrets). [#167978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167978), [3d0c697e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d0c697eba0ff29875458652ebf6273f6b7cea32). Thanks [@MarkMa84](https://github.com/MarkMa84), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

</details>

### Quality-of-Life Improvements

- Child stops retain completed cancellations through overlapping updates or later cleanup failures. Swarm Stop waits for execution and cleanup, which can remain pending after terminal status appears. After a timeout or incomplete result, inspect remaining children with `subagents action=list` and retry; replacement work needs its own Stop. Failed registration retains the child IDs for inspection before retrying. See [child operations](/tools/subagents/operations).
- Interrupted replies can still use completed tool results. A silent command timeout can notify its conversation when automatic completion wake is enabled. Check external effects before repeating work. See [background-process handling](/gateway/background-process).
- Conversation controls preserve other agents' replies and queued work when short session keys overlap. Files, images and usage reports stay with the selected conversation, and clearing export search restores the sidebar. See [session controls](/concepts/session).
- Adding a clarification mid-turn preserves the first executable tool or parallel batch; unstarted sequential calls can still be skipped. Use `/stop` or `/queue interrupt` to cancel. Cloud-worker context with unsupported media asks you to stop or reclaim the session and retry locally. See [steering](/concepts/queue-steering) and [transcript handling](/reference/transcript-hygiene).
- Common command errors explain how to correct or recover the request. Missing results remain unknown, so inspect current state before retrying an action with side effects. `gateway call` checks connection settings; use `config validate` for a full configuration check. See [Gateway calls](/cli/gateway/query), [command arguments](/tools/exec) and [optional tool-loop detection](/tools/loop-detection).
- A disconnected terminal send keeps your unsent draft. [Custom usage footers](/concepts/usage-tracking) refresh after repeated saves, and existing [blocker answers](/tools/progress-card) no longer need redundant replacement explanations. Older ACPX file-state imports and the legacy Copilot URL rewrite are removed.
- Tool-heavy conversations retain fewer duplicate results and discarded strings; reusable readers release completed requests. **Plugin and SDK callers must treat transcript payloads as immutable as soon as they submit them, even while an asynchronous save is pending.** Create new values for later updates. Hooks receive saved rewritten activity and omit suppressed entries. See the [SDK runtime guide](/plugins/sdk-runtime).
- More history, usage and session bookkeeping runs in background database workers, leaving less storage work on the thread handling requests. Confirmed saves publish once; uncertain outcomes stay pending or return recovery guidance. See the [worker-access reference](/reference/database-schemas/worker-access).
- Workspace saves and recovery wait for durable records before changing files or reporting success. Stock persona files stay in the [configured agent workspace](/concepts/system-prompt) when work runs in a separate project.

Completed native subagent results can return after a user follow-up under the original caller's permissions. An ended child's delayed delivery also need not hold its parent's completed batch indefinitely; active work still waits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Resume an unfinished built-in-runtime conversation after compaction followed by a Gateway restart, preserving the original request once and retaining distinct queued follow-ups. [#160128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160128). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explain when recent messages exceed the context limit and suggest a new session with a brief summary. [#160934](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160934). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Keep completed background tool results available for final answers and empty-answer retries, and keep internal finalization prompts out of user-message history. [#161091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161091). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Finish stopping a child during an overlapping session metadata update, preserving both the update and recorded stop across retry or restart. Cleanup still checks ownership before affecting replacement sessions. [#161461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161461). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let stopped collector children finish recording completion and free their slot during simultaneous session metadata writes, allowing queued work to start while preserving labels and delivery identity. [#161676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161676). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Record the owning agent when cancelling a watched child with an unqualified session key, preserving other agents’ running work and queued messages under the same key. Older ownerless records still use current configuration. [#162540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162540). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Return the accepted terminal cancellation when stopping a child overlaps a session metadata update, while refusing to publish an old run’s outcome into a replacement session. [#163096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163096). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid an unnecessary preparation write when stopping a Swarm child whose session is already ready. Pending changes for that session and final cancellation publication still finish in their normal order. [#163485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163485). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Make Swarm Stop wait for the selected children’s execution and queued-launch cleanup and report cleanup failures. Terminal task status can appear before cleanup finishes; after a timeout or incomplete cancellation, inspect remaining children with subagents action=list and retry. [#163558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163558). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resume the originating conversation after a silent background-command timeout when automatic completion wake is enabled. Check external effects before retrying non-idempotent work. [#164171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164171). Thanks to [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Save worker transcripts through symbolic links or directory junctions using the admitted native database location, avoiding the path-alias persistence failure after response generation. [#164289](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164289). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Distinguish confirmed session removal from incomplete or skipped cleanup when handling Stop. [28885d3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/28885d3f8745f51e39ecb54389283dc391c15381). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retain counts of child cancellations already committed when later Stop cleanup fails, and show the cleanup error. Inspect remaining runs with subagents; replacement work requires its own Stop request. [6123d17](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6123d17b9f281f07f0074c564f6ffa20deec50ae). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let cleanup save a child cancellation it has accepted instead of competing with a sweeper save. [e9571d7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e9571d77e76bd6d35996273d9e8398ad539b26e1). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep child cancellation and its saved abort marker consistent during overlapping metadata updates without stopping replacement work. [fef7992](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fef7992b3697b5f56a3f469ec951ec210b8918b4). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep responsibility for cancelling the exact child after required registration fails. Errors retain child session/run IDs and pending-cleanup status; capacity stays reserved until cleanup settles, so inspect the retained child before retrying. Session-maintenance planning also runs separately from archive waits. [7555e3f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7555e3f88132bc5915e42141d9a09aa435ed7c2a). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid a false permanent cancellation error when subagent metadata is still being saved after selected children have stopped. Wait for that metadata before continuing tree discovery. [cf5b871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cf5b87163b768a011611e085d2356ad32213cb07). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep run files and completion records attached to their original agent and conversation after active run context clears. [#161119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161119). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share a pending local identity lookup across simultaneous Codex and Session Share discovery requests, reducing duplicate startup database work without changing discovered nodes and sessions. Missing or failed identity reads remain retryable. [#161496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161496). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid duplicate validation when Gateway clients establish warm message subscriptions, while preserving access checks and approval replay. [#161555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161555). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve another agent’s active reply, queued messages and commands when stopping, deleting, resetting or archiving a conversation with a colliding session key. The selected agent’s own work is still cancelled. [#161736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161736). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve other agents’ queued replies and commands during review pauses, workspace placement changes, rewinds, broad aborts and interrupts sharing a short session key. Broad cleanup removes only the selected agent’s own work across session incarnations. [#162179](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162179). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepare requested session-list rows before rebuilding unrelated rows after metadata changes, so overlapping requests can share preparation. [#162280](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162280). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Omit nested tool-result cards from recalled history unless includeTools is explicitly true. [#162904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162904). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Keep session owners and participant details visible after background updates, preserve newer overlapping changes and publish committed updates despite an observer error. [#163030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163030). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Find a run’s files and images in its original owning conversation after session aliases change, while explicit session selection still follows current aliases. Current visibility checks apply, and changed or replaced sessions return a retryable failure. [#163054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163054). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read usage summaries, charts, logs and costs from the selected transcript or validated store, refusing stale or mixed source locations. [#163182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163182). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserve valid shared-session access while restoring archived history, avoiding an unnecessary retry when forking, rewinding or switching branches. [#163669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163669). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save accepted messages and steering input once with current approved content when database paths use aliases or a session manager is reused. [#163753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163753). Thanks to [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Keep the first executable tool and parallel tool batches when a clarification arrives mid-turn, delivering real results before the new message. After a sequential tool starts, remaining unstarted calls can still be skipped; use /stop or /queue interrupt to cancel work. [#163852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163852). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse session ordering for repeated large lists as metadata and filters change. [#163941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163941). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore the full sidebar in HTML conversation exports when Escape clears search, and display malformed stored read-range values as literal text. Valid numeric ranges retain their display. [#163997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163997). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep session usage and context-weight reports available when an unrelated database registration changes during their read, while still checking the selected store and agent attribution. [#164217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164217). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep replacement subscribers receiving activity, context, model and reconnect updates when an old unsubscribe repeats. Remove temporary HTTP listeners after an immediate bind failure. [#164221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164221). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repair the session-tool guide’s link to Gateway timeout examples, which explain caller wait separately from the receiving agent’s execution budget. [0338bb3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0338bb3adbafa24f8887e169634169ae60d690af). Thanks to [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
- Release a deleted agent’s model, authentication, memory and database resources without restarting the Gateway. Recreated agents can use external session stores again, while deletion preserves the existing choice to keep files and protects surviving agents’ stores. [40d284f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/40d284f9a6aaa6b11f6d9d7bc1db253781d7ef76). Thanks to [@ceckert](https://github.com/ceckert), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep literal internal status blocks out of streamed and final replies, and keep application context attached to the correct steering message. Cloud-worker context with unsupported media now explicitly asks you to stop or reclaim the session and retry locally. [cd9913f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cd9913f1b1105f9c4e7b96fde8c1431a77e5878d). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks [@Bartok9](https://github.com/Bartok9) for the earlier empty-snapshot direction.
- Let simultaneous first-session requests for a fresh agent share database creation, avoiding false replacement errors when another request creates the same store first. [36507e7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/36507e78d6ef52dc58fa66cfd51494a8e5481458). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the original session-creation repair.
- Reject unsupported process wait arguments with instructions to use `timeout`, keeping output available for a corrected request. [#153844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153844). Thanks to [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Reject unsupported `exec` working-directory arguments before a command runs, with guidance to use `workdir`. [#154248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154248). Thanks to [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Recognize repeated tool calls rejected for invalid arguments when tool-loop detection is enabled, so the agent can correct the request. [#158709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158709). Thanks to [@ali-log](https://github.com/ali-log), [@51Google](https://github.com/51Google), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Preserve unusual keybinding names and their string or array overrides when loading and reloading them. [#161749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161749). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move repetitive recurring-job starts from debug to trace, retaining useful diagnostic history. Use trace logging to see every recurring start; one-shot starts and failures keep their existing levels. [#161895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161895). Thanks to [@matthematics1137](https://github.com/matthematics1137), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Honor an explicit session-creation transport timeout in either fallback branch, including inherited spawn policy. Bundled callers using default or null deadlines retain their existing waits. [#161969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161969). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Guide agents to report requested undelivered results, meaningful changes and unresolved problems, while staying quiet about already-handled background results. [#162302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162302). Thanks to [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Keep manual result-collection reminders in running process status, logs and lists when completion notifications were disabled at command launch. Structured followUp and no-exit-wake labels remain until completion; execution and notification policy stay unchanged. [#162303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162303). Thanks to [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Avoid command-cleanup modules for affected version queries and package-root library imports. [#162311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162311). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@love-ai-open-source](https://github.com/love-ai-open-source).
- Preserve unrelated paths when shortening home directories in tool details and CLI diagnostics, including containers whose home is the filesystem root. [#162483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162483). Thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Avoid a false blocked-tool warning when steering skips a pending tool that never started. [#162513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162513). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY), [@dori-jitter](https://github.com/dori-jitter), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Select the intended local transcription program when a home-relative PATH entry passes through a symbolic link and its parent, preserving configured precedence and home directories containing PATH separators. [#162680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162680). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Refresh file-based custom usage footers after repeated editor saves that replace the file, including deletion and recreation. Refresh still depends on filesystem notifications, which an open old-file handle can delay on Linux. [#162733](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162733). Thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco).
- Preserve intended emoji boundaries on affected Bun runtimes when splitting messages or truncating terminal text, avoiding unnecessary cuts that waste the available space. [#162749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162749). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep POSIX sibling paths containing literal backslashes absolute in CLI diagnostics while shortening actual home-directory descendants. [#162800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162800). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco).
- Guide agents to leave a note with the unfinished task, blocker, owner and resume condition when paused work has no authorized next step. [#162903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162903). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Keep unsent slash-prefixed terminal chat after a disconnected send is rejected, preserve newer drafts and allow retry after reconnecting. [#163201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163201). Thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Treat a missing tool result as an unknown outcome and guide agents to check current state before repeating actions with side effects. Warn operators when request history changes unexpectedly. [#163379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163379). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Give common chat, terminal and Control UI errors a short explanation and recovery step, with expandable details. Keep uncertain dispatched outcomes, manual database recovery and Stop-saving warnings visible. [#163381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163381). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Restore specific recovery guidance for disconnected Codex devices, outdated workers and offline runners. [#163538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163538). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explain that an allowed nested configuration parameter is unset and point to config set instead of calling it unknown. The message does not establish provider support for that parameter. [#163682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163682). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cap oversized log-follow intervals so timer overflow cannot turn a long wait into rapid Gateway polling. [#163791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163791). Thanks to [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
- Show the Gateway’s specific error when a visible child session fails startup or registration, including unconfirmed cleanup. [#163798](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163798). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Let Bun source installations launch from external agent workspaces, retain the selected agent in shared-store usage reports, and keep admitted child completion work tracked through graceful restart or suspension. [#163821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163821). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore standard MIT license recognition while keeping the same terms and third-party notices available through the README and package. [#163838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163838). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen). Thanks [@ashleywolf](https://github.com/ashleywolf) for reporting the license-recognition problem.
- Observe model-preparation failures while the terminal UI is idle, preventing an extra unhandled-rejection report while retaining the original error. [#163842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163842). Thanks to [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Suppress late approval messages after terminal shutdown. Older ACPX file-state imports and the legacy Copilot-host-to-Codex URL rewrite are removed. [#163854](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163854). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Send literal `constructor` and `__proto__` text to interactive processes even when cursor-key mode is unknown. [#163963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163963). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep queued voice, Talk and upload diagnostics attached to the submitting request instead of a preceding task’s trace. [#164105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164105). Thanks to [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Close the temporary agent and shared databases before deleting one-shot agent exec state, including when worker registration finishes late. [#164483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164483). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Preserve an answer that already reports results and explains a concrete approval, input or dependency blocker. Its completion check can finish silently while authorized unfinished work still receives the check. [#164896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164896). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Start replies in the current session working directory, using the configured default if its override was cleared. [863e377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/863e377524a970787dfb2540b32722810c512cff). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Make Gateway CLI option links jump to their descriptions on the running, query and discovery reference pages. [1edf7ea](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1edf7ea3ab5dd59c9ed6091c030a232504b5a58d). Thanks to [@qingminglong](https://github.com/qingminglong).
- Give `config patch` refusals usable `--replace-path` retry advice, including punctuation and quotes in provider names. Show separate bash/zsh and PowerShell spellings when needed. [e6b3a11](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e6b3a11851de205f5139ea4ea33051409ad36ebb). Thanks to [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Avoid unused CLI startup imports and let `gateway call` validate connection settings without checking unrelated configuration. Use `config validate` for a full check. Preserve literal resolved credentials and report cleanup permission failures after identifying a listener. [01d59ff](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/01d59ffc5392541eed49b5c57cad93077c93013a). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce repeated copying when selecting child sessions that must be protected during maintenance. [#161526](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161526). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Copy only relevant child-run records for control and prompt context, avoiding unrelated tasks in large histories. [#161690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161690). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse database workers on macOS and Linux Bun builds that pass native SQLite cleanup checks. Stock Bun 1.4.2, Windows and inconclusive checks retain conservative worker retirement. [#161764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161764). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Load fewer unrelated modules when starting session, history and usage readers. [#162609](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162609). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share preparation for session updates sent to several clients and avoid repeated secret-filter checks within one output pass. [#162810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162810). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release catalog listeners and disconnected-client caches after stalled discovery or reconnects, while continuing to track genuinely unfinished native work. [#163688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163688). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid copying the old transcript while validating and adopting concurrent history updates. [#163710](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163710). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid rebuilding full session lists or copying unrelated details during lifecycle actions in large stores. Background storage handoffs can still add to total request time. [#163815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163815). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share immutable published session and worker facts instead of repeatedly copying them for inventory and placement reads. [#164102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164102). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retain less unnecessary data from settled tools, private Code Mode results and published child records during long tool-heavy runs. Persist trajectory events in batches within the existing rolling retention limit. [#164199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164199). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share submitted transcript data to retain fewer duplicate tool results. Plugin and SDK callers must treat payloads as immutable immediately, including while an asynchronous save is pending, and create new values for updates. [#164251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164251). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release discarded large tool output when a transcript keeps only a short excerpt, including excerpts carried through prepared redaction metadata. [#164361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164361). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release completed read requests and their prompt or transcript data from reusable SQLite read workers while keeping those workers available for later reads. [#164370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164370). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release duplicate completed tool data and finished-run state during long Code Mode sessions, retaining saved history and the five-minute warm-worker window. Lifecycle hooks receive saved rewritten activity and omit suppressed entries. [#164386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164386). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse frozen configuration captures during reply and tool preparation instead of repeatedly copying unchanged settings. Active turns retain their admitted policy and new work receives updated settings. [#164398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164398). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retain less nested transcript bookkeeping and share compiled validators for equivalent schemas. Schema contents determine reuse; the SDK’s `cacheKey` no longer selects it. [#164467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164467). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share equal immutable data between initial history and model context, including incognito sessions, while keeping private fields outside the model’s view. [#164502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164502). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let eligible idle history readers release their database using their recorded capability, and defer the default heap-limit probe until memory diagnostics need it. [9137cfc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9137cfcc5f4e2a3a6cac458590b491c1031b535a). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce transcript-worker imports to the board and progress-card schemas they use, avoiding the broader Gateway protocol dependency. [a02d441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a02d441dbf8aa40a13b6be0a18c5f2b1f15bc77e). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) for the original transcript-worker repair.
- Read shared GitHub publication status and recovery records in database workers, reducing storage work on the Gateway request thread. [#161214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161214). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce repeated database checks for cached state, profile access and session listings while detecting schema changes and revoked ownership. [#161678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161678). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gather cloud-worker idle-suspension candidates in a database worker while retaining the existing suspension policy and pending-recovery exclusions. [#161849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161849). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gather remote-worker disk-monitor candidates in a database worker while retaining the existing probes and disk warnings. [#161878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161878). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resolve requester profiles in database workers for the covered metadata, model-list, search and profile requests, checking current access before effects. [#162414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162414). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep newer upstream-session links intact when delayed monitoring results arrive, preserve retry progress after a failed event write, and continue processing other provider results. [#162518](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162518). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Run conversation archive checks in database workers before writing durable history. [#162521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162521). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gather health and status session summaries in database workers while preserving counts, recent-session order and current store ownership. [#162625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162625). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save remote-worker acknowledgments in database workers before continuing workspace-result recovery. [#162975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162975). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let the Gateway handle other pending work between session-list preparation batches, while concurrent callers still share preparation and receive the same ordered visible results. [#163031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163031). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read archived-session status in database workers and send restore notifications only after a successful settled restore. [#163041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163041). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Check attachment ownership in database workers and recheck current session access and download tickets before returning protected media. [#163049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163049). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read long histories for recovery, usage totals and MCP App restoration in database workers, publishing prepared messages after commit. [#163053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163053). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepare covered agent and recovery session reads in database workers, rejecting delayed results after cancellation or session replacement. [#163115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163115). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Copy and relink restart-interrupted conversations in database workers while keeping recovery atomic. [#163119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163119). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move completion and reply-preparation transcript reads into database workers, adopting committed messages before notifying observers. [#163120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163120). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid scanning every saved session key when checking an agent-specific store during session creation. [#163163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163163). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepare covered conversation lists, routing addresses and manual-compaction bindings in database workers. [#163208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163208). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save and prune durable usage-report cache data in database workers, keeping refresh locks and known lost-reply recovery without repeating writes. [#163287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163287). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move selected routine session-metadata transactions into database workers and publish after confirmed commits. Lost worker replies do not repeat committed writes. [#163378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163378). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read session versions, bounded history, update watches and notice acknowledgments in database workers without losing queued events during a handoff to the same database. [#163513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163513). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read shared-session suggestions in a database worker and filter them by current access before returning them. [#163546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163546). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read durable pending messages for chat history and startup in database workers, and retain confirmed interruption status after a lost worker reply. [#163582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163582). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move worker-transcript replay bookkeeping into database workers while preserving sequence order and duplicate detection. Unknown writes stay pending for recovery. [#163594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163594). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read saved history for compaction, reset hooks, side questions, exports and asynchronous SDK consumers in database workers. Worker failure does not fall back to a blocking durable read. [#163605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163605). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save supported transcript, queued-input, session and Goal changes together in background storage work. Publish confirmed saves once; unknown outcomes stop without repeating writes. [#163889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163889). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep checking incognito workers when disk-backed session preparation fails, leaving affected disk sessions unknown rather than treating them as absent. [#163972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163972). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Batch database-schema metadata reads instead of repeating queries for every table and index. [#164077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164077). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid serializing unchanged prompt, skill and diff snapshots during routine metadata updates, while fully saving changed or cleared snapshots. [#164093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164093). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Check board membership once per session-list batch instead of once per row, using one committed snapshot for the results. [#164094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164094). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Track conversation-environment activity with one fewer database read per touch while retaining current attachment and ownership checks. [#164108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164108). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Use indexed ownership checks for already-admitted state handles while retaining conservative checks for other connections. [#164110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164110). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse bounded prepared database statements while each query still reads current data. [#164117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164117). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read needed activity and lifecycle details without loading unused saved prompts, skills or diff baselines. [#164125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164125). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save pending chat and agent input in background storage work. Final outcomes and cleanup wait for accepted saves; SDK callers can explicitly await completion without replaying an uncertain write. [#164284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164284). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepare watched-conversation context in background storage work with current visibility checks. Plugin authors can await the new authority-bound helper; the synchronous helper remains supported. [#164288](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164288). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Record session creation, compaction and child-launch signals, and prune lifecycle history in database workers. [#164367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164367). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Remove or restore saved Codex and Agents API session bindings in background storage work, blocking reuse after uncertain outcomes. [#164391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164391). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move selected chat writer claims, model-switch updates and final-delivery bookkeeping into database workers. [#164514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164514). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reread selected session-creation metadata in a database worker while checking current routing and intervening writes. [#165360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165360). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allow concurrent first transcript writes to create a new session store without mistaking another writer’s creation for a replacement. [922d989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/922d98926bb27143c812be35f9a918c1e48b045a). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid a repeated database admission read during cold-transcript preparation. [0853228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/08532288490cc7f07543d4254d03fcd5aa1b09d3). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Run durable single-session reset storage in a database worker and publish acknowledged resets after a lost reply without repeating their writes. [850c49c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/850c49ccec1b918e0eaa7aa6fe26f37b527d3f01). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep new stock persona templates in the configured agent workspace when direct, child or session-bound scheduled work runs in a separate project. The project retains its working directory and AGENTS.md instructions; existing files remain. [#161088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161088). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@nikosml](https://github.com/nikosml).
- Store workspace save and recovery journals in database workers. Wait for durable records before changing files or reporting success; uncertain commits retain files and recovery records. [#161539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161539). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepare workspace snapshots, aliases, setup updates and expiry cleanup in background storage work. Plugin authors can adopt a SQLite-free `guard.assertHost`; `beforePersistentApply` remains supported until the next SDK major. [#163496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163496). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share Git discovery for matching concurrent opt-in observed-project requests. Default project listing is unchanged, and a slow bulk discovery can still delay unrelated metadata reads. [#162016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162016). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Completed delegated work can return to the original conversation after a bounded wait for an ended child that has not delivered its result. Active children still wait, and a late child result may arrive separately. See [subagent announcements](/tools/subagents/announce). [#165987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165987). Thanks [@LifeViwer](https://github.com/LifeViwer), [@mgr-nick](https://github.com/mgr-nick), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [@vinsew](https://github.com/vinsew).
- Sending a follow-up while native subagents work no longer prevents their completed results from reaching the original conversation. Delivery retains the original caller's permissions; revoked access still stops it. See [subagent handoffs](/concepts/subagent-yield-handoff). [#168068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168068). Thanks [@A1fred-AI](https://github.com/A1fred-AI), [@adamamzalag](https://github.com/adamamzalag), [@klabir](https://github.com/klabir), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333), [@stackingrockss](https://github.com/stackingrockss), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

### Other Bug Fixes

- A Linux command that runs out of file handles fails without stopping other streaming commands. On restrictive Linux hosts using Node and matching native host and worker builds, cleanup can release a worker for the next turn despite denied process-group signals. If cleanup cannot be confirmed, the worker remains unavailable; restarting alone does not clear that condition. Cloud-worker moves and dispatches can recover a finished result without waiting for a timeout.
- Cancelling worker chat lets the current turn clean up without another provider call. Startup cleanup remains retryable when a supervisor disappears before its broker is ready. Shutdown attempts worker-connection and transfer cleanup even when inventory is unreadable, then reports that error.
- Credential saves preserve a paired host's launch reply. Linux cleanup no longer mistakes the covered exited tasks for running ones; permission errors remain inconclusive. Saved workspace-recovery errors retain the readable process diagnostics shown on screen.
- Repository sessions retain saved workspace identity, and unrelated writes preserve new session-state events. Lost category-edit replies no longer repeat unrelated reads. Temporary database contention before work starts gets bounded retries; workers lost before dispatch can be replaced without resending the turn. Doctor warnings show underlying failures.
- Snapshot cleanup preserves replacements at reused paths. The covered staging, lock and rollback failures retain their original errors while valid cleanup finishes. If the snapshot-management process is lost, unresolved files remain protected until service recovery. Restarting it alone does not make manual deletion safe; consult the [database recovery guidance](/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery).
- Native Incognito conversations delete successfully. Archives, pending inputs and transcript checks recognize aliases for the same database. Session discovery retains valid Incognito and placement information despite unrelated registry changes or invalid disk candidates.
- Scheduled turns and Control UI chats on native Windows avoid the affected session-read errors. Commands and workers can skip an optional compile cache when its long path is unsafe. If an externally set `NODE_COMPILE_CACHE` hangs before OpenClaw starts, use `NODE_DISABLE_COMPILE_CACHE=1`. SQLite inspection subprocesses also preserve explicit recognized Node compiler settings.
- Commands containing a literal NUL are rejected before helpers start or running work is replaced, with correction guidance. Valid multiline commands remain supported in the [exec tool](/tools/exec). On POSIX systems, `config patch --file` and `config set --batch-file` reject named pipes without a writer instead of waiting indefinitely; regular files and symlinks remain supported.
- Configuration errors show which file failed validation and why. If an external edit invalidates or removes the file while a change is being applied, the request reports an error and keeps the last working runtime settings. Repair the file, call `config.get`, then reapply the change. Pairing lists skip malformed entries, reject invalid schema type lists, and keep existing system-agent approval details.
- Network updates fix compressed responses and redirects while preserving Slack proxy compatibility. Replaced skill folders can be watched again, with polling fallback available. Doctor retains loaded native plugin files after cleanup.
- Finished chats, old display rows, disconnected tool subscriptions and short previews release unneeded data without interrupting accepted work. Non-Windows Bash and Zsh caches retain recent shell snapshots. Abandoned HTTP responses can release buffers when garbage collection runs; SDK callers should still finish or cancel response bodies for prompt cleanup.

Session operations avoid circular database waits when usage refresh overlaps other work. Expired requests can leave waiting queues, and cancellation does not undo an operation already accepted.

- [Interrupted database reads retain snapshots still in use until their original owner finishes cleanup](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160946). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Malformed pairing-list entries no longer abort the whole read, invalid schema type lists are rejected, and SDK readers retain existing system-agent approval details.](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161054). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Repository sessions retain their committed workspace identity when a reply is lost, and cleanup preserves replacement database files](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161124). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- [Database contention before a snapshot directory is created reports the original retry error without a false cleanup failure](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161372). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- [Worker cancellation can finish when the last native operation ends between cleanup checks](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/646a3bdaf60ba92986935d09a36798c2d189bf58). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Linux process checks confirm an exit when a status file disappears between checks, without treating permission errors as proof of exit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/112df95f5ee65a0b17622735e2b616d2910dc79b). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- [Transcript and question-answer receipt checks accept equivalent paths to the same database, including filesystem aliases](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/32d49385f67f09054c8d9b03f10e0e7f3c68d33b). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

- Conversation delivery, history, Boards and related session operations avoid circular database waits when usage refresh overlaps other work, allowing requests to finish or return their intended errors. [#167662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167662). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Bun Gateways and CLI commands retain access to shared state when a new renewal worker starts with a different elapsed-time origin. A clock change that puts a renewal in the future still interrupts ownership and can require a Gateway restart. See [Gateway locks](/gateway/gateway-lock). [#167669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167669). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Core and twelve extensions update their filesystem helper to the revision selected to retain readable handles for cloned destinations. [a808a7e4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a808a7e4f8f64ccbfc61ce916478afc892f9171e). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Ordinary worker callbacks count against the task's original timeout. Cancelled storage requests can leave the queue before they start without disrupting other callers; cancellation does not undo work already accepted. [e15a4e97](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e15a4e971938774d890612a45a0386b9c6b99276). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Expired history and usage requests stop their waiting work under database contention. Cancelled usage writes no longer run later when the queue clears. [0965e459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0965e45991cbf457ef89ed44dfdf2bfdec644008). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retiring an imported session source no longer lets its archived content be imported again into a replacement conversation. [eff7e0b4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/eff7e0b4a26889fe2ee42c8f62d9167220cc5079). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
